// @earendil-works/chord — virtual module shim (chord2 portable kernel + delta).
//
// Upstream `packages/chord` (chord 1.2.0, the `0104fef1e` "replace the facet and
// service runtime with the chord2 host runtime" rewrite; regenerated at
// `3daa20280` including the wire generation of
// `90f17f59a`/`018c87ddc`/`db6497c3d` — schema-derived frame
// family, `ack` + end-to-end flow control, deflated >64 KiB payloads) is a
// hot-reloadable object/host runtime: `object`s (state + code at an address),
// `handle`s, principals, observable JSON state (snapshots + deltas), memory/IPC/
// WebSocket links, and a TypeBox-runtime contract vocabulary
// (`local`/`remote`/`method`/`opaque` + `cell`/`derivation`/`listener`/`resource`
// declarations). Only `src/loader.ts` and `src/manager.ts` import `node:*`
// (fork/IPC child hosts, `Module.registerHooks`, the net process lock) and
// `src/link.ts` type-imports `node:zlib` for the payload codecs (bound to this
// runtime's `node:zlib` virtual module, see Environment notes); the rest is pure
// JS whose only dependency is `typebox` (this runtime's existing shim).
//
// Package entries (upstream `package.json` exports), all served by THIS module:
//   .         src/index.ts        createHost/defineFacet/hostPrincipal, contracts,
//                                 declarations, replicatedState, links, json, errors
//   ./context src/context/index.ts BACKGROUND_CONTEXT, createContextKey, withCancel, ...
//   ./delta   src/delta/index.ts  track/apply/applyImmutable/applyImmutableBatches/
//                                 diffRevisions/encoder/decoder/assertValidOp/...
//   ./node    src/node.ts         THROWS — see `pi_chord_node_shim.js.txt`
// (`/context` and `/delta` are named subsets of this one module's surface, exactly
// as `/declarations` and `/portable` are subsets of the pi-codemode shim; every
// upstream runtime export of the three entries is present, and the union adds the
// other two entries' names — importing a name from the "wrong" entry therefore
// links here where upstream would refuse. Nothing else is exported.)
//
// What is REAL here
//   * The whole portable kernel, ported statement by statement: `contract.ts`
//     (description/fingerprint/`coverage`/`bind`/accepts adapters), `declare.ts`,
//     `errors.ts`, `json.ts` (strict-JSON copy/validate), `schema.ts`
//     (canonicalJson/hash64/`fits`/normalize/canonicalIdentity), `cell.ts`
//     (Source/Cell/Derived, the publication queue, dependent recomputation),
//     `record.ts` (Self, resources, listeners, tasks, registrations, handles,
//     activation/unwind/disposal, factory reruns), `host.ts` (epochs, records,
//     roots, registries, routes, `accept`/`connect`, retire/dispose ordering),
//     `connection.ts` (hold/call/sub/cancel/state framing per connection, delta
//     encoding per subscription, relays, epochs/fencing, the `ack`-based window
//     that coalesces waiting state per subscription, `maxCalls`), `remote.ts`
//     (RemoteHandle + remote state reception), `reload.ts` (prepare/commit/abort,
//     gates, migration, rollback), `cluster.ts` (parent/child reload
//     reconciliation over links), `wire.ts` (the single FrameSchema the types are
//     derived from — including `ack` — + error mapping), `link.ts` (memory/IPC/
//     WebSocket links, frame sizes for flow control, deflated payload packing at
//     `PACK_THRESHOLD`), `registry.ts`, `websocket.ts`, `state.ts`
//     (replicatedState: authoritative mutable + publication-only attached source,
//     delivery ordering/overflow).
//   * `delta/**`: the transactional JSON tracker (`track`/`beginChange`/
//     `prepare`/`adopt`/`prepareReplace`, draft proxies, array piece tree, op
//     emission), `diff.ts`, `apply-immutable-trusted.ts`, `tracker.ts`,
//     `revision-validator.ts`, and the codec (`encoder`/`decoder`) —
//     `chord/delta` is a standalone tracker with no host dependency.
//   * `context/index.ts` in full.
//
// What THROWS
//   * `@earendil-works/chord/node` only (`js/pi_chord_node_shim.js.txt`): every
//     entry point throws `ERR_NOT_SUPPORTED`. pir has no `fork`/IPC child-host
//     transport, no `node:module` `registerHooks`, and no net-address process
//     lock; the extension host is single-process. Upstream's own loader also
//     documents that its in-process reload leaks every old module copy, so the
//     facet-module loader is not something to emulate. Loading facets from module
//     paths therefore needs `createHost({ loader })` with a custom in-process
//     loader (`Facet` objects and `FacetLoader` are ordinary values here — the
//     upstream tests' in-process loader is what the ported cases use).
//
// How this file is derived (mechanical, no hand edits inside a module body)
//   * Every upstream `src/**` module except `loader.ts`/`manager.ts`/`node.ts` is
//     type-stripped (`node:module` `stripTypeScriptTypes`, mode "strip", which
//     blanks annotations in place and so keeps upstream line/column positions),
//     then concatenated in dependency order into ONE shared module scope with the
//     `// ══ <path> ══` banner of its own source file. Relative imports/re-exports
//     disappear (the names already live in that scope); the only surviving import
//     is `typebox`/`typebox/value`, which the runtime resolves to the typebox
//     shims. The `export {}` block at the end is the union of the three entries'
//     upstream export lists (type-only names dropped), so the shipped surface is
//     exactly `index.ts ∪ context/index.ts ∪ delta/index.ts`.
//   * Nine module-private helper names collide across module scopes and were
//     renamed inside the module that owned them (nothing else changed):
//     `DATA_DESCRIPTOR`/`defineData` keep their names in
//     apply-immutable-trusted.ts (json.ts: `jsonDATA_DESCRIPTOR`/
//     `jsonDefineData`; tracker.ts: `trackerDATA_DESCRIPTOR`/
//     `trackerDefineData`), `isContainer` keeps its name in tracker.ts
//     (apply-immutable-trusted.ts: `trustedIsContainer`; diff.ts:
//     `diffIsContainer`; revision-validator.ts: `validatorIsContainer`),
//     `MAX_DELTA_OPERATIONS` keeps it in tracker.ts (diff.ts:
//     `diffMAX_DELTA_OPERATIONS`), `emitSet` keeps it in diff.ts (tracker.ts:
//     `trackerEmitSet`), `noop` keeps it in record.ts (reload.ts: `reloadNoop`;
//     remote.ts: `remoteNoop`), the `Reload` marker schema keeps it in
//     wire.ts (reload.ts's class: `reloadReload`), and `encoder`/`decoder` keep
//     their names in delta/index.ts (link.ts's `TextEncoder`/`TextDecoder`
//     instances: `linkEncoder`/`linkDecoder`).
//
// Environment notes (pir vs upstream Node)
//   * `AbortSignal.any`, `queueMicrotask`, `structuredClone`, `WeakRef`, `Proxy`
//     and `Object.hasOwn` are all present in this runtime (host globals), so the
//     cancellation/delivery machinery runs unmodified.
//   * Schema validation goes through the typebox shim + the host JSON-Schema
//     subset validator (`__pir_schemaValidate`). `not`, `contains` and
//     `patternProperties` (what `Type.Record` emits) are modelled as of
//     2026-10-14 — self-tests and fuzz pass; the corpus re-run status is per
//     the foreman gate record. Everything else
//     (type/const/enum/anyOf/oneOf/allOf/items/required/properties/
//     additionalProperties/constraints) is enforced. `Value.Default`,
//     `Value.Clean` and `Type.Record` originally behaved differently and were
//     corrected in `typebox_shim.js.txt`/`typebox_value_shim.js.txt` for this
//     contract (see those files). `Type.Immutable` (the new wire.ts `List`
//     wrapper) is the same gap: the typebox shim now carries it as the identity
//     it is in typebox.
//   * `node:zlib` is imported at the head of this file instead of upstream's
//     `process?.getBuiltinModule?.("node:zlib")`: this runtime's
//     `getBuiltinModule` registry is keyed by short names and throws on a miss,
//     so the unguarded upstream lookup would throw at module load here. The two
//     functions are the same `deflateRawSync`/`inflateRawSync` pair (over the
//     host flate2 bridge), and the rest of upstream's packing code is unchanged
//     (`zlib` stays the object its `=== undefined` guards test). The browser-only
//     `DecompressionStream` fallback in `inflate` therefore never runs here.
//   * `/node`-free but still Node-ish: `remoteAddress`/wire frames are pure JSON;
//     no `process` access outside the throwing `/node` stub, and `node:zlib` is
//     the only non-typebox import.

import { Type } from "typebox";
import { Value } from "typebox/value";
import { deflateRawSync, inflateRawSync } from "node:zlib";
// ══ errors.ts ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
/** Error codes callers see (guide table). A refused or aborted reload keeps the code of its cause. */
const CHORD_ERROR_CODES = [
	"aborted",
	"contract_mismatch",
	"forbidden",
	"gone",
	"invalid",
	"not_in_variant",
	"not_ready",
	"released",
	"superseded",
	"unavailable",
	"upgrade_conflict",
	"upgrade_required",
]         ;
                                                                

/**
 * Finer cause inside a code, for logs and tests: e.g. `invalid` + `args`, `result`, `state`, `input`, `behavior`,
 * `duplicate`, `cycle`, `read_only`, `not_eager`, `registry_conflict`; `unavailable` + `unknown_type`.
 */
                                      

const RETRYABLE                              = new Set(["superseded", "upgrade_conflict"]);

class ChordError extends Error {
	         code                ;
	         reason                              ;
	         retryable         ;
	         details         ;
	constructor(
		code                ,
		message        ,
		options                                                                    ,
	) {
		super(`${code}${options?.reason ? `/${options.reason}` : ""}: ${message}`, {
			cause: options?.cause,
		});
		this.name = "ChordError";
		this.code = code;
		this.reason = options?.reason;
		this.retryable = RETRYABLE.has(code);
		this.details = options?.details;
	}
}

const isChordError = (error         , code                 )                      =>
	error instanceof ChordError && (code === undefined || error.code === code);

/** For factories and methods refusing a principal: `throw forbidden()`. */
const forbidden = (message = "forbidden")             => new ChordError("forbidden", message);

const invalid = (reason                  , message        , details          )             =>
	new ChordError("invalid", message, { reason, details });

/** Keeps a ChordError's code; anything else becomes `unavailable` with the original as cause. */
const asChordError = (error         , context        )             =>
	error instanceof ChordError
		? error
		: new ChordError("unavailable", `${context}: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`, {
				cause: error,
			});

/** Runs every step, then throws one error, or an AggregateError when several failed. */
async function runAll(steps                            , message        )                {
	const errors            = [];
	for (const step of steps) {
		try {
			await step();
		} catch (error) {
			errors.push(error);
		}
	}
	if (errors.length === 1) throw errors[0];
	if (errors.length > 1) throw new AggregateError(errors, message);
}

// ══ schema.ts ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
/** JSON Schema as data. TypeBox schemas are plain JSON Schema objects. */
                                                  

const asSchema = (schema         )         =>
	schema !== null && typeof schema === "object" ? (schema          ) : schema === false ? { not: {} } : {};

/** Canonical JSON: sorted object keys, `undefined` members dropped. */
function canonicalJson(value         )         {
	if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value) ?? "null";
	if (Array.isArray(value)) return `[${value.map((item) => canonicalJson(item)).join(",")}]`;
	const keys = Object.keys(value)
		.filter((key) => (value                           )[key] !== undefined)
		.sort();
	return `{${keys.map((key) => `${JSON.stringify(key)}:${canonicalJson((value                           )[key])}`).join(",")}}`;
}

/** Non-cryptographic 64-bit hash (two FNV-1a lanes). Contracts are trusted, so collisions are not an attack surface. */
function hash64(text        )         {
	let a = 0x811c9dc5;
	let b = 0x01000193 ^ 0x5bd1e995;
	for (let index = 0; index < text.length; index++) {
		const code = text.charCodeAt(index);
		a = Math.imul(a ^ code, 0x01000193);
		b = Math.imul(b ^ code, 0x5bd1e995) ^ (b >>> 13);
	}
	return (a >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0") + (b >>> 0).toString(16).padStart(8, "0");
}

function deepEqual(a         , b         )          {
	return a === b || canonicalJson(a) === canonicalJson(b);
}

/** Throws `invalid` (with `reason`) and the first schema errors when `value` does not match. */
function assertValid(reason                  , what        , schema         , value         )       {
	if (Value.Check(schema, value)) return;
	const errors = [...Value.Errors(schema, value)].slice(0, 3);
	const summary = errors.map((error) => `${error.instancePath || "/"} ${error.message}`).join("; ");
	throw invalid(reason, `${what}: ${summary}`, errors);
}

/** Applies defaults, strips unknown fields, validates. Returns a fresh value. */
function normalize(schema         , value         , what        )          {
	const normalized = Value.Clean(schema, Value.Default(schema, Value.Clone(value ?? {})));
	assertValid("input", what, schema, normalized);
	return normalized;
}

/** Canonical identity of a normalized value: fields equal to their schema default are omitted. */
function canonicalIdentity(schema         , value         )         {
	return canonicalJson(stripDefaults(asSchema(schema), value));
}

function stripDefaults(schema        , value         )          {
	if (Array.isArray(value)) {
		const items = schema.items;
		if (Array.isArray(items)) return value.map((item, index) => stripDefaults(asSchema(items[index]), item));
		return value.map((item) => stripDefaults(asSchema(items), item));
	}
	if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return value;
	const properties = asSchema(schema.properties);
	const result                          = {};
	for (const [key, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
		const property = asSchema(properties[key]);
		if ("default" in property && deepEqual(property.default, item)) continue;
		result[key] = stripDefaults(property, item);
	}
	return result;
}

const CONSTRAINTS = [
	"minLength",
	"maxLength",
	"pattern",
	"format",
	"minimum",
	"maximum",
	"exclusiveMinimum",
	"exclusiveMaximum",
	"multipleOf",
	"minItems",
	"maxItems",
	"uniqueItems",
	"minProperties",
	"maxProperties",
]         ;

const typesOf = (schema        )                       =>
	typeof schema.type === "string" ? [schema.type] : Array.isArray(schema.type) ? (schema.type            ) : undefined;

/** Keywords `fits` reasons about. Any other validation keyword in the provider's schema forces exact equality. */
const KNOWN                      = new Set([
	...CONSTRAINTS,
	"type",
	"properties",
	"required",
	"additionalProperties",
	"patternProperties",
	"items",
	"additionalItems",
	"const",
	"enum",
	"anyOf",
	"parameters",
	"returnType",
]);

/** Annotations: no effect on validity. */
const ANNOTATIONS                      = new Set([
	"title",
	"description",
	"default",
	"examples",
	"$id",
	"$schema",
	"$comment",
	"readOnly",
	"writeOnly",
	"deprecated",
]);

const withoutAnnotations = (schema        )         =>
	Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(schema).filter(([key]) => !ANNOTATIONS.has(key)));

/**
 * Conservative structural subtyping: true only if every value valid under `sub` is valid under `sup`, where object
 * fields missing from `sub` but optional in `sup` count as fine (unknown fields are stripped at the provider, see
 * `RecordImpl.call`). A provider schema using a keyword this function does not model (`not`, `allOf`, `contains`,
 * `$ref`, ...) or an `additionalProperties` schema only fits an identical consumer schema.
 */
function fits(subSchema         , supSchema         )          {
	const sub = withoutAnnotations(asSchema(subSchema));
	const sup = withoutAnnotations(asSchema(supSchema));
	if (Object.keys(sup).length === 0) return true;
	if (deepEqual(sub, sup)) return true;
	if (Object.keys(sup).some((key) => !KNOWN.has(key))) return false;
	if (sup.additionalProperties !== undefined && typeof sup.additionalProperties !== "boolean") return false;
	if (Array.isArray(sub.anyOf)) return sub.anyOf.every((branch) => fits(branch, sup));
	if (Array.isArray(sup.anyOf)) return sup.anyOf.some((branch) => fits(sub, branch));
	if ("const" in sup) return "const" in sub && deepEqual(sub.const, sup.const);
	if (Array.isArray(sup.enum)) {
		const values = "const" in sub ? [sub.const] : Array.isArray(sub.enum) ? sub.enum : undefined;
		return values?.every((item) => (sup.enum             ).some((allowed) => deepEqual(allowed, item))) ?? false;
	}
	for (const key of CONSTRAINTS) if (key in sup && !deepEqual(sub[key], sup[key])) return false;
	const supTypes = typesOf(sup);
	const subTypes = typesOf(sub);
	if (supTypes === undefined || subTypes === undefined) return false;
	if (!subTypes.every((type) => supTypes.includes(type) || (type === "integer" && supTypes.includes("number")))) {
		return false;
	}
	if ("const" in sub || Array.isArray(sub.enum)) return true;
	if (subTypes.includes("object") && !objectFits(sub, sup)) return false;
	if (subTypes.includes("array") && !arrayFits(sub, sup)) return false;
	if (subTypes.includes("function")) return functionFits(sub, sup);
	return true;
}

function objectFits(sub        , sup        )          {
	const subProperties = asSchema(sub.properties);
	const supProperties = asSchema(sup.properties);
	const subRequired = new Set((sub.required                        ) ?? []);
	for (const key of (sup.required                        ) ?? []) if (!subRequired.has(key)) return false;
	for (const [key, property] of Object.entries(supProperties)) {
		if (key in subProperties) {
			if (!fits(subProperties[key], property)) return false;
		} else if (typeof sub.additionalProperties === "object" && !fits(sub.additionalProperties, property)) {
			// The consumer may send this name with any value its `additionalProperties` allows. Open consumer objects
			// are fine: unknown names are stripped with the consumer's own schema before the call (`RecordImpl.call`).
			return false;
		}
	}
	if (sup.additionalProperties === false) {
		if (sub.additionalProperties !== false) return false;
		if (Object.keys(subProperties).some((key) => !(key in supProperties))) return false;
	}
	const supPatterns = asSchema(sup.patternProperties);
	if (Object.keys(supPatterns).length > 0) {
		// Every value the consumer may put under a matching name must fit, and the consumer must not allow arbitrary
		// extra names (it would then allow any value under a matching one).
		const subPatterns = asSchema(sub.patternProperties);
		if (sub.additionalProperties !== false && Object.keys(subPatterns).length === 0) return false;
		for (const [pattern, property] of Object.entries(supPatterns)) {
			const matcher = new RegExp(pattern, "u");
			for (const [key, subProperty] of Object.entries(subProperties)) {
				if (!(key in supProperties) && matcher.test(key) && !fits(subProperty, property)) return false;
			}
			if (
				Object.keys(subPatterns).length > 0 &&
				(!(pattern in subPatterns) || !fits(subPatterns[pattern], property))
			) {
				return false;
			}
		}
		for (const pattern of Object.keys(subPatterns)) if (!(pattern in supPatterns)) return false;
	}
	return true;
}

function arrayFits(sub        , sup        )          {
	if (Array.isArray(sup.items)) {
		if (!Array.isArray(sub.items) || sub.items.length !== sup.items.length) return false;
		return sub.items.every((item, index) => fits(item, (sup.items             )[index]));
	}
	if (Array.isArray(sub.items)) return sub.items.every((item) => fits(item, sup.items));
	return fits(sub.items, sup.items);
}

function functionFits(sub        , sup        )          {
	const subParameters = (sub.parameters                         ) ?? [];
	const supParameters = (sup.parameters                         ) ?? [];
	// A function accepting fewer parameters is fine; each shared parameter is contravariant.
	if (subParameters.length > supParameters.length) return false;
	if (!subParameters.every((parameter, index) => fits(supParameters[index], parameter))) return false;
	return fits(sub.returnType, sup.returnType);
}

// ══ contract.ts ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const MEMBER = Symbol("chord.member");

                                                                                       
	                            
	                 
	                   
 

                                          
	                            
	                    
	                                    
	                  
 

const method =                                       (args   , result   )                   => ({
	[MEMBER]: "method",
	args,
	result,
});

/** A native in-process object identified by `name@version` (local contracts only). */
const opaque =              (id        )                => {
	if (!/^[^@\s]+@\d+$/.test(id)) throw new TypeError(`opaque id must be name@version: ${id}`);
	return { [MEMBER]: "opaque", id };
};

                             
	                                                   
	                                                        
 

                            
	                         
	                        
	                                                        
 

/** A local member: a method, an opaque native object, or a state member described by its schema. */
                                                            

                           
	                         
	                        
	                                                         
 

// A description travels as a hold's contract token (wire.ts), so it is declared once, as the schema that validates it.
// `TSchema` is an empty interface: any object is one, which is all a peer's schema is checked for here.
const SchemaObject = Type.Unsafe         (Type.Object({}));

const MemberDescriptionSchema = Type.Union([
	Type.Object({ kind: Type.Literal("method"), args: SchemaObject, result: SchemaObject }),
	Type.Object({ kind: Type.Literal("state"), schema: SchemaObject }),
	Type.Object({ kind: Type.Literal("opaque"), id: Type.String() }),
]);
                                                                       

/** Local contracts have one unnamed variant. */
const LOCAL_VARIANT = "";

const DescriptionSchema = Type.Object({
	name: Type.String({ minLength: 1 }),
	scope: Type.Union([Type.Literal("remote"), Type.Literal("local")]),
	version: Type.Integer(),
	input: SchemaObject,
	variants: Type.Record(Type.String(), Type.Record(Type.String(), MemberDescriptionSchema)),
});
                                                           

                        
	                      
	                         
	                        
	                                  
	                             
 

                                                                                       
	                         
	                
 

                                                                                    
	                        
	                
 

                                                         

/** Names a handle uses itself; members may not use them. */
const RESERVED_MEMBERS                      = new Set([
	"kind",
	"status",
	"ready",
	"shape",
	"error",
	"onShape",
	"onStatus",
	"release",
	"then",
	"contract",
]);

function describeMember(name        , member         , scope                    )                    {
	if (RESERVED_MEMBERS.has(name)) throw new TypeError(`member name is reserved: ${name}`);
	const brand = (member                         )[MEMBER];
	if (brand === "method") {
		const spec = member              ;
		return { kind: "method", args: spec.args, result: spec.result };
	}
	if (brand === "opaque") {
		if (scope === "remote") throw new TypeError(`remote contracts cannot expose opaque members: ${name}`);
		return { kind: "opaque", id: (member              ).id };
	}
	return { kind: "state", schema: member            };
}

function finish                       (contract                        )    {
	return { ...contract, fingerprint: hash64(canonicalJson(contract.description)) }     ;
}

function checkHeader(name        , version        )       {
	if (!name) throw new TypeError("contract name is empty");
	if (!Number.isInteger(version) || version < 1) throw new TypeError(`contract version must be a positive integer`);
}

function remote                           (name        , def   )                    {
	checkHeader(name, def.version);
	const variants                                                    = {};
	if (Object.keys(def.variants).length === 0) throw new TypeError(`remote contract ${name} has no variants`);
	for (const [kind, variant] of Object.entries(def.variants)) {
		const members                                    = {};
		for (const [key, schema] of Object.entries(variant.state ?? {}))
			members[key] = describeMember(key, schema, "remote");
		for (const [key, spec] of Object.entries(variant.methods ?? {})) {
			if (key in members) throw new TypeError(`member ${key} is both state and method in ${name}.${kind}`);
			members[key] = describeMember(key, spec, "remote");
			if (members[key].kind !== "method") throw new TypeError(`methods.${key} is not a method()`);
		}
		variants[kind] = members;
	}
	const description              = { name, scope: "remote", version: def.version, input: def.input, variants };
	return finish                   ({
		scope: "remote",
		name,
		version: def.version,
		input: def.input,
		def,
		description,
	});
}

function local                          (name        , def   )                   {
	checkHeader(name, def.version);
	const members                                    = {};
	for (const [key, member] of Object.entries(def.members ?? {})) members[key] = describeMember(key, member, "local");
	const description              = {
		name,
		scope: "local",
		version: def.version,
		input: def.input,
		variants: { [LOCAL_VARIANT]: members },
	};
	return finish                  ({ scope: "local", name, version: def.version, input: def.input, def, description });
}

/**
 * A consumer token received over a link (only its description travels). Has no `def`, so it is only good for binding
 * and validation, never for TypeScript types.
 */
function contractFromDescription(description             )              {
	const { name, scope, version, input } = description;
	if (typeof name !== "string" || (scope !== "remote" && scope !== "local") || !Number.isInteger(version)) {
		throw new ChordError("invalid", "malformed contract token", { reason: "token" });
	}
	const base = { name, version, input, description, fingerprint: hash64(canonicalJson(description)) };
	return scope === "remote"
		? { ...base, scope, def: { version, input, variants: {} } }
		: { ...base, scope, def: { version, input } };
}

/** Returns why `provider` does not cover `consumer`, or undefined when it does. */
function coverage(provider             , consumer             )                     {
	if (provider.name !== consumer.name || provider.scope !== consumer.scope) return "different type";
	if (!fits(consumer.input, provider.input)) return "input";
	for (const [kind, consumerMembers] of Object.entries(consumer.variants)) {
		const providerMembers = provider.variants[kind];
		if (providerMembers === undefined) return `variant ${kind} missing`;
		for (const [name, wanted] of Object.entries(consumerMembers)) {
			const offered = providerMembers[name];
			if (offered === undefined || offered.kind !== wanted.kind) return `${kind}.${name} missing or of another kind`;
			if (offered.kind === "method" && wanted.kind === "method") {
				if (!fits(wanted.args, offered.args)) return `${kind}.${name} arguments`;
				if (!fits(offered.result, wanted.result)) return `${kind}.${name} result`;
			} else if (offered.kind === "state" && wanted.kind === "state") {
				if (!fits(offered.schema, wanted.schema)) return `${kind}.${name} state`;
			} else if (offered.kind === "opaque" && wanted.kind === "opaque" && offered.id !== wanted.id) {
				return `${kind}.${name} opaque ${offered.id} != ${wanted.id}`;
			}
		}
	}
	return undefined;
}

/** Maps the provider's current behavior to the view a consumer of an older contract version expects. */
                                                                                    
                                                        

/** How a consumer token binds to a provider: directly or through an `accepts` adapter. Throws contract_mismatch. */
function bind(provider             , accepts                     , consumer             )                      {
	if (provider.fingerprint === consumer.fingerprint) return undefined;
	if (provider.version === consumer.version) {
		const reason = coverage(provider.description, consumer.description);
		if (reason === undefined) return undefined;
		throw new ChordError(
			"contract_mismatch",
			`${provider.name}@${provider.version} does not cover the consumer's description (${reason}); forgotten version bump?`,
		);
	}
	const adapter = accepts?.[consumer.version];
	if (adapter === undefined) {
		throw new ChordError(
			"contract_mismatch",
			`${provider.name}: provider version ${provider.version}, consumer version ${consumer.version}, no accepts adapter`,
		);
	}
	return adapter;
}

/** What a factory returns for contract C. */
                                                             
	                                                                                           
	                                  
		                                                                                                              
		        

/** Late-bound members a handle exposes for contract C (all variants merged). */

// ══ declare.ts ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Declaration descriptors. A factory passes them in object literals; the property name identifies the declaration
// across factory reruns (reload), independent of call order.

const DECL = Symbol("chord.decl");

                                  
	                                                    
 

                              
	                        
	                         
	                    
 

                                  
	                                                       
	                     
	                                                                                       
	                                   
	                                                                                  
	                             
	                                                                
	                                       
 

                                  
	                            
	                               
 

                              
	                     
	                                     
	                  
 

                               
	                            
	                         
	                                                                       
	                                                                
 

                                    
	                              
	                          
	                                       
 

/** A state cell: schema (required; there is no TypeScript at runtime) and initial value (validated). */
const cell =                    (schema   , initial           )                      => ({
	[DECL]: "cell",
	schema,
	initial,
});

const resource =    (spec                 )                  => ({ [DECL]: "resource", spec });

                                                      
                                                                                                         

/**
 * Reacts to a source once the record is live. `listener(source, fn)` subscribes to the source itself: a readable
 * (cell, derivation, handle state member: called with each value, starting with the current one), an event source,
 * or a resource whose value is one. `listener(source, select, fn)` subscribes to `select(value)`, where value is the
 * opened resource for a resource source. Each event runs `fn` in a fresh root.
 */
                                                                                                                
                               
	          
	                                               
	                                                 
                
function listener(source         , ...rest                    )               {
	const [select, fn] = rest.length === 1 ? [undefined, rest[0]] : [rest[0], rest[1]];
	return {
		[DECL]: "listener",
		source,
		select: select                          ,
		fn: fn                      ,
	};
}

const derivation =                             (
	sources   ,
	fn                                   ,
)                    => ({ [DECL]: "derivation", sources, fn: fn                                         });

const declKind = (value         )                     =>
	value !== null && typeof value === "object" ? (value                       )[DECL] : undefined;

// ══ publish.ts ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// One FIFO queue for every observer notification (state events, shape pushes, registry tables, listener
// invocations).
//
// Problem: notifying observers synchronously from inside a write lets a re-entrant write overtake the write being
// published. Example: subscriber A reacts to n = 1 by writing n = 2; subscriber B then sees 2 before 1.
// Solution: a publication first adopts every revision it makes (cells and derivations), then enqueues its
// notifications. Notifications run in enqueue order, so every observer sees revisions in adoption order.
// `holdPublications` defers delivery until a multi-step change (a reload commit) is complete.

const queue                 = [];
let held = 0;
let draining = false;

/** Runs `task` after everything enqueued before it. Tasks must not throw (report instead). */
function enqueue(task            )       {
	queue.push(task);
	drain();
}

/** Delivers nothing until `fn` returns, then everything it enqueued, in order. */
function holdPublications   (fn         )    {
	held++;
	try {
		return fn();
	} finally {
		held--;
		drain();
	}
}

function drain()       {
	if (draining || held > 0) return;
	draining = true;
	try {
		for (let task = queue.shift(); task !== undefined; task = queue.shift()) task();
	} finally {
		draining = false;
	}
}

// ══ delta/diff.ts ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const DEFAULT_OVERLAP_SCAN = 65_536;
const diffMAX_DELTA_OPERATIONS = 4_096;
const overflowedBatches = new WeakSet      ();

const emitOperation = (operations      , operation    )       => {
	if (overflowedBatches.has(operations)) return;
	if (operations.length >= diffMAX_DELTA_OPERATIONS) {
		overflowedBatches.add(operations);
		return;
	}
	operations.push(operation);
};

const diffIsContainer = (value           )                                                   =>
	value !== null && typeof value === "object";

const emitSet = (path      , value           , operations      )       => {
	if (path.length === 0) emitOperation(operations, ["r", value]);
	else emitOperation(operations, ["s", path                , value]);
};

const equalJson = (left           , right           )          => {
	if (left === right) return true;
	if (!diffIsContainer(left) || !diffIsContainer(right) || Array.isArray(left) !== Array.isArray(right)) return false;
	if (Array.isArray(left)) {
		const other = right               ;
		if (left.length !== other.length) return false;
		for (let index = 0; index < left.length; index++) {
			if (!equalJson(left[index] , other[index] )) return false;
		}
		return true;
	}
	const other = right                             ;
	const keys = Object.keys(left);
	if (keys.length !== Object.keys(other).length) return false;
	for (const key of keys) {
		if (!Object.hasOwn(other, key) || !equalJson(left[key] , other[key] )) return false;
	}
	return true;
};

const permutation = (before                      , after                      )                       => {
	if (before.length !== after.length) return undefined;
	const positions = new Map                                                ();
	for (let index = 0; index < before.length; index++) {
		const value = before[index] ;
		const entry = positions.get(value);
		if (entry === undefined) positions.set(value, { indices: [index], used: 0 });
		else entry.indices.push(index);
	}
	const result = new Array        (after.length);
	for (let index = 0; index < after.length; index++) {
		const entry = positions.get(after[index] );
		if (entry === undefined || entry.used === entry.indices.length) return undefined;
		result[index] = entry.indices[entry.used++] ;
	}
	return result;
};

const emitString = (before        , after        , path              , operations      )       => {
	if (before === after) return;
	if (after.length > before.length && after.slice(0, before.length) === before) {
		emitOperation(operations, ["a", path, after.slice(before.length)]);
		return;
	}
	const shared = overlap(before, after, DEFAULT_OVERLAP_SCAN);
	if (shared === 0) {
		emitOperation(operations, ["s", path, after]);
		return;
	}
	emitOperation(operations, ["t", path, before.length - shared]);
	if (after.length > shared) emitOperation(operations, ["a", path, after.slice(shared)]);
};

                                                           

                       
	               
	              
	                 
  

const MAX_IDENTITY_CANDIDATES = 200_000;
const MAX_SEMANTIC_CELLS = 65_536;

const sameValue = (left           , right           )          => left === right || equalJson(left, right);

const lcsMatches = (
	before                      ,
	after                      ,
	equal                                                ,
	maxCells        ,
)                           => {
	if (before.length === 0 || after.length === 0) return [];
	if (before.length * after.length > maxCells) return undefined;
	const width = after.length + 1;
	const lengths = new Uint32Array((before.length + 1) * width);
	for (let left = before.length - 1; left >= 0; left--) {
		for (let right = after.length - 1; right >= 0; right--) {
			const at = left * width + right;
			lengths[at] = equal(before[left] , after[right] )
				? lengths[(left + 1) * width + right + 1]  + 1
				: Math.max(lengths[(left + 1) * width + right] , lengths[left * width + right + 1] );
		}
	}
	const matches               = [];
	let left = 0;
	let right = 0;
	while (left < before.length && right < after.length) {
		if (
			equal(before[left] , after[right] ) &&
			lengths[left * width + right] === lengths[(left + 1) * width + right + 1]  + 1
		) {
			matches.push([left++, right++]);
		} else if (lengths[(left + 1) * width + right]  >= lengths[left * width + right + 1] ) left += 1;
		else right += 1;
	}
	return matches;
};

const semanticallyAligned = (left           , right           )          => {
	if (sameValue(left, right)) return true;
	if (!diffIsContainer(left) || !diffIsContainer(right) || Array.isArray(left) !== Array.isArray(right)) return false;
	if (Array.isArray(left) && Array.isArray(right)) {
		if (left.length !== right.length) return false;
		return left.some((value, index) => diffIsContainer(value) && value === right[index]);
	}
	const leftObject = left                             ;
	const rightObject = right                             ;
	for (const key of Object.keys(leftObject)) {
		const value = leftObject[key];
		if (diffIsContainer(value) && Object.hasOwn(rightObject, key) && value === rightObject[key]) return true;
	}
	return false;
};

const lowerBound = (values                   , value        )         => {
	let low = 0;
	let high = values.length;
	while (low < high) {
		const middle = (low + high) >>> 1;
		if (values[middle]  < value) low = middle + 1;
		else high = middle;
	}
	return low;
};

const identitySubsequence = (
	before                      ,
	beforeStart        ,
	beforeEnd        ,
	after                      ,
	afterStart        ,
	afterEnd        ,
)                           => {
	const beforeCount = beforeEnd - beforeStart;
	const afterCount = afterEnd - afterStart;
	const matches               = [];
	if (afterCount < beforeCount) {
		let beforeIndex = beforeStart;
		for (let afterIndex = afterStart; afterIndex < afterEnd; afterIndex++) {
			while (beforeIndex < beforeEnd && before[beforeIndex] !== after[afterIndex]) beforeIndex += 1;
			if (beforeIndex === beforeEnd) return undefined;
			matches.push([beforeIndex++, afterIndex]);
		}
		return matches;
	}
	if (beforeCount < afterCount) {
		let afterIndex = afterStart;
		for (let beforeIndex = beforeStart; beforeIndex < beforeEnd; beforeIndex++) {
			while (afterIndex < afterEnd && before[beforeIndex] !== after[afterIndex]) afterIndex += 1;
			if (afterIndex === afterEnd) return undefined;
			matches.push([beforeIndex, afterIndex++]);
		}
		return matches;
	}
	return undefined;
};

const greedyIdentityAnchors = (
	positions                                           ,
	after                      ,
	afterStart        ,
	afterEnd        ,
)               => {
	const matches               = [];
	let previous = -1;
	for (let afterIndex = afterStart; afterIndex < afterEnd; afterIndex++) {
		const candidates = positions.get(after[afterIndex] );
		if (candidates === undefined) continue;
		const at = lowerBound(candidates, previous + 1);
		const beforeIndex = candidates[at];
		if (beforeIndex === undefined) continue;
		matches.push([beforeIndex, afterIndex]);
		previous = beforeIndex;
	}
	return matches;
};

const identityAnchors = (
	before                      ,
	beforeStart        ,
	beforeEnd        ,
	after                      ,
	afterStart        ,
	afterEnd        ,
)               => {
	const positions = new Map                     ();
	for (let index = beforeStart; index < beforeEnd; index++) {
		const value = before[index] ;
		const existing = positions.get(value);
		if (existing === undefined) positions.set(value, [index]);
		else existing.push(index);
	}
	let candidateCount = 0;
	for (let index = afterStart; index < afterEnd; index++) {
		candidateCount += positions.get(after[index] )?.length ?? 0;
		if (candidateCount > MAX_IDENTITY_CANDIDATES) {
			return greedyIdentityAnchors(positions, after, afterStart, afterEnd);
		}
	}
	if (candidateCount === 0) return [];

	const candidates                   = [];
	const tails           = [];
	const tailValues           = [];
	for (let afterIndex = afterStart; afterIndex < afterEnd; afterIndex++) {
		const beforePositions = positions.get(after[afterIndex] );
		if (beforePositions === undefined) continue;
		for (let index = beforePositions.length - 1; index >= 0; index--) {
			const beforeIndex = beforePositions[index] ;
			const at = lowerBound(tailValues, beforeIndex);
			const candidateIndex = candidates.length;
			candidates.push({ before: beforeIndex, after: afterIndex, previous: at === 0 ? -1 : tails[at - 1]  });
			tails[at] = candidateIndex;
			tailValues[at] = beforeIndex;
		}
	}
	const matches               = [];
	let candidateIndex = tails[tails.length - 1] ?? -1;
	while (candidateIndex >= 0) {
		const candidate = candidates[candidateIndex] ;
		matches.push([candidate.before, candidate.after]);
		candidateIndex = candidate.previous;
	}
	matches.reverse();
	return matches;
};

const processArrayMatches = (
	before             ,
	after             ,
	path      ,
	operations      ,
	beforeStart        ,
	beforeEnd        ,
	afterStart        ,
	afterEnd        ,
	outputStart        ,
	matches                       ,
)       => {
	let beforeAt = beforeStart;
	let afterAt = afterStart;
	let outputAt = outputStart;
	for (const [beforeMatch, afterMatch] of matches) {
		if (overflowedBatches.has(operations)) return;
		diffArrayRegion(before, after, path, operations, beforeAt, beforeMatch, afterAt, afterMatch, outputAt);
		outputAt += afterMatch - afterAt;
		if (!sameValue(before[beforeMatch] , after[afterMatch] )) {
			diffValue(before[beforeMatch] , after[afterMatch] , [...path, outputAt], operations);
		}
		outputAt += 1;
		beforeAt = beforeMatch + 1;
		afterAt = afterMatch + 1;
	}
	if (!overflowedBatches.has(operations)) {
		diffArrayRegion(before, after, path, operations, beforeAt, beforeEnd, afterAt, afterEnd, outputAt);
	}
};

function diffArrayRegion(
	before             ,
	after             ,
	path      ,
	operations      ,
	beforeStart        ,
	beforeEnd        ,
	afterStart        ,
	afterEnd        ,
	outputStart        ,
)       {
	if (overflowedBatches.has(operations)) return;
	while (beforeStart < beforeEnd && afterStart < afterEnd && sameValue(before[beforeStart] , after[afterStart] )) {
		beforeStart += 1;
		afterStart += 1;
		outputStart += 1;
	}
	while (beforeStart < beforeEnd && afterStart < afterEnd && sameValue(before[beforeEnd - 1] , after[afterEnd - 1] )) {
		beforeEnd -= 1;
		afterEnd -= 1;
	}
	const beforeCount = beforeEnd - beforeStart;
	const afterCount = afterEnd - afterStart;
	if (beforeCount === 0 && afterCount === 0) return;
	if (beforeCount === 0 || afterCount === 0) {
		emitOperation(operations, ["p", path, outputStart, beforeCount, after.slice(afterStart, afterEnd)]);
		return;
	}

	if (beforeCount === afterCount) {
		const positional               = [];
		for (let offset = 0; offset < beforeCount; offset++) {
			if (sameValue(before[beforeStart + offset] , after[afterStart + offset] )) {
				positional.push([beforeStart + offset, afterStart + offset]);
			}
		}
		if (positional.length > 0) {
			processArrayMatches(
				before,
				after,
				path,
				operations,
				beforeStart,
				beforeEnd,
				afterStart,
				afterEnd,
				outputStart,
				positional,
			);
			return;
		}
	}

	const subsequence = identitySubsequence(before, beforeStart, beforeEnd, after, afterStart, afterEnd);
	if (subsequence !== undefined && subsequence.length > 0) {
		processArrayMatches(
			before,
			after,
			path,
			operations,
			beforeStart,
			beforeEnd,
			afterStart,
			afterEnd,
			outputStart,
			subsequence,
		);
		return;
	}

	const identity = identityAnchors(before, beforeStart, beforeEnd, after, afterStart, afterEnd);
	if (identity.length > 0) {
		processArrayMatches(
			before,
			after,
			path,
			operations,
			beforeStart,
			beforeEnd,
			afterStart,
			afterEnd,
			outputStart,
			identity,
		);
		return;
	}

	const semantic = lcsMatches(
		before.slice(beforeStart, beforeEnd),
		after.slice(afterStart, afterEnd),
		semanticallyAligned,
		MAX_SEMANTIC_CELLS,
	);
	if (semantic !== undefined && semantic.length > 0) {
		const absolute = semantic.map(
			([beforeIndex, afterIndex]) => [beforeStart + beforeIndex, afterStart + afterIndex]         ,
		);
		processArrayMatches(
			before,
			after,
			path,
			operations,
			beforeStart,
			beforeEnd,
			afterStart,
			afterEnd,
			outputStart,
			absolute,
		);
		return;
	}

	if (beforeCount === 1 && afterCount === 1) {
		diffValue(before[beforeStart] , after[afterStart] , [...path, outputStart], operations);
		return;
	}
	emitOperation(operations, ["p", path, outputStart, beforeCount, after.slice(afterStart, afterEnd)]);
}

const diffArray = (before             , after             , path      , operations      )       => {
	if (before === after || equalJson(before, after)) return;
	if (
		before.length === after.length &&
		before.length > 1 &&
		!sameValue(before[0] , after[0] ) &&
		!sameValue(before[before.length - 1] , after[after.length - 1] )
	) {
		const order = permutation(before, after);
		if (order !== undefined) {
			emitOperation(operations, ["m", path, order]);
			return;
		}
	}
	diffArrayRegion(before, after, path, operations, 0, before.length, 0, after.length, 0);
};

const diffObject = (
	before                           ,
	after                           ,
	path      ,
	operations      ,
)       => {
	const beforeKeys = Object.keys(before);
	const afterKeys = Object.keys(after);
	if ([...beforeKeys, ...afterKeys].some((key) => RESERVED_SEGMENTS.has(key))) {
		if (!equalJson(before, after)) emitSet(path, after, operations);
		return;
	}
	for (const key of afterKeys) {
		if (overflowedBatches.has(operations)) return;
		if (Object.hasOwn(before, key)) diffValue(before[key] , after[key] , [...path, key], operations);
		else emitSet([...path, key], after[key] , operations);
	}
	for (const key of beforeKeys) {
		if (overflowedBatches.has(operations)) return;
		if (!Object.hasOwn(after, key)) emitOperation(operations, ["d", [...path, key]                           ]);
	}
};

const diffValue = (before           , after           , path      , operations      )       => {
	if (before === after || overflowedBatches.has(operations)) return;
	if (typeof before === "string" && typeof after === "string" && path.length > 0) {
		emitString(before, after, path                , operations);
		return;
	}
	if (Array.isArray(before) && Array.isArray(after)) {
		diffArray(before, after, path, operations);
		return;
	}
	if (diffIsContainer(before) && diffIsContainer(after) && !Array.isArray(before) && !Array.isArray(after)) {
		diffObject(before, after, path, operations);
		return;
	}
	emitSet(path, after, operations);
};

const jsonCost = (value           )         => {
	if (value === null) return 4;
	if (typeof value === "string") return value.length + 2;
	if (typeof value === "number") return String(value).length;
	if (typeof value === "boolean") return value ? 4 : 5;
	if (Array.isArray(value)) {
		let cost = 2;
		for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index++) cost += jsonCost(value[index] ) + (index === 0 ? 0 : 1);
		return cost;
	}
	let cost = 2;
	let index = 0;
	for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
		cost += key.length + 3 + jsonCost(value[key] ) + (index++ === 0 ? 0 : 1);
	}
	return cost;
};

const pathCost = (path      )         => {
	let cost = 2;
	for (let index = 0; index < path.length; index++) {
		const segment      = path[index] ;
		cost += (typeof segment === "string" ? segment.length + 2 : String(segment).length) + (index === 0 ? 0 : 1);
	}
	return cost;
};

const operationCost = (operation    )         => {
	switch (operation[0]) {
		case "r":
			return 6 + jsonCost(operation[1]);
		case "s":
			return 7 + pathCost(operation[1]) + jsonCost(operation[2]);
		case "d":
			return 6 + pathCost(operation[1]);
		case "a":
			return 7 + pathCost(operation[1]) + operation[2].length + 2;
		case "t":
			return 7 + pathCost(operation[1]) + String(operation[2]).length;
		case "p":
			return (
				10 +
				pathCost(operation[1]) +
				String(operation[2]).length +
				String(operation[3]).length +
				jsonCost(operation[4])
			);
		case "m":
			return 7 + pathCost(operation[1]) + jsonCost(operation[2]);
	}
};

/** Compute a compact operation batch from two immutable JSON revisions. */
function diffRevisions(before           , after           )       {
	const operations       = [];
	diffValue(before, after, [], operations);
	if (overflowedBatches.has(operations)) return [["r", after]];
	if (operations.length === 0 || operations[0]?.[0] === "r") return operations;
	let deltaCost = 2;
	for (const operation of operations) deltaCost += operationCost(operation) + 1;
	if (deltaCost < 65_536) return operations;
	const snapshotCost = jsonCost(after) + 6;
	return deltaCost >= snapshotCost ? [["r", after]] : operations;
}

// ══ json.ts ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
/** Strict-JSON representation of an application data type. Unknown payloads become JsonValue. */
                                                         
	           
	                   
		           
		                                            
			   
			                                    
				                             
				                  
					                                                    
					        

const jsonDATA_DESCRIPTOR                     = {
	value: undefined,
	writable: true,
	enumerable: true,
	configurable: true,
};

                               
	                                                                                
	                                           
  

/** Copy a value into an alias-free strict-JSON tree owned by the caller. */
function copyJson(value         , options                  )            {
	return copy(value, undefined, options?.omitUndefinedProperties === true);
}

function copy(value         , ancestors                         , omitUndefinedProperties         )            {
	if (value === null || typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "boolean") return value;
	if (typeof value === "number") {
		if (Number.isFinite(value)) return value;
		throw new TypeError("Value contains a non-finite number and is not strict JSON");
	}
	if (typeof value !== "object")
		throw new TypeError(`Value contains a non-JSON ${typeof value}; expected strict JSON`);
	const active = ancestors ?? new Set        ();
	if (active.has(value)) throw new TypeError("Value contains cycles and is not strict JSON");
	active.add(value);
	try {
		if (Array.isArray(value)) {
			// Indices plus `length` only: extra, symbol, and missing keys all change the count.
			if (Object.getPrototypeOf(value) !== Array.prototype || Reflect.ownKeys(value).length !== value.length + 1) {
				throw new TypeError("Value must contain strict JSON dense plain arrays");
			}
			const result = new Array           (value.length);
			for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index++) {
				const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, index);
				if (descriptor === undefined || !descriptor.enumerable || !("value" in descriptor)) {
					throw new TypeError("Value must contain strict JSON enumerable indexed data properties");
				}
				jsonDefineData(result, String(index), copy(descriptor.value, active, omitUndefinedProperties));
			}
			return result;
		}
		const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
		if (prototype !== Object.prototype && prototype !== null) {
			throw new TypeError("Value must contain strict JSON plain objects or arrays");
		}
		const result = Object.create(prototype)                             ;
		for (const key of Reflect.ownKeys(value)) {
			if (typeof key === "symbol") throw new TypeError("Value contains a symbol key and is not strict JSON");
			const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, key) ;
			if (!descriptor.enumerable || !("value" in descriptor)) {
				throw new TypeError("Value must contain strict JSON enumerable data properties");
			}
			if (descriptor.value === undefined && omitUndefinedProperties) continue;
			jsonDefineData(result, key, copy(descriptor.value, active, omitUndefinedProperties));
		}
		return result;
	} finally {
		active.delete(value);
	}
}

function jsonDefineData(target        , key             , value           )       {
	jsonDATA_DESCRIPTOR.value = value;
	Object.defineProperty(target, key, jsonDATA_DESCRIPTOR);
	jsonDATA_DESCRIPTOR.value = undefined;
}

/** Return whether a value is finite strict JSON with plain objects and no cycles. */
function isJsonValue(value         )                     {
	return check(value, new Set        ());
}

function check(value         , ancestors             )          {
	if (value === null || typeof value === "string" || typeof value === "boolean") return true;
	if (typeof value === "number") return Number.isFinite(value);
	if (Array.isArray(value)) {
		if (Object.getPrototypeOf(value) !== Array.prototype) return false;
		const keys = Reflect.ownKeys(value);
		if (keys.length !== value.length + 1 || keys.some((key) => typeof key !== "string")) return false;
		if (ancestors.has(value)) return false;
		ancestors.add(value);
		try {
			for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index++) {
				const descriptor = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(value, String(index));
				if (
					descriptor === undefined ||
					!descriptor.enumerable ||
					!("value" in descriptor) ||
					!check(descriptor.value, ancestors)
				) {
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		} finally {
			ancestors.delete(value);
		}
	}
	if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return false;
	const prototype = Object.getPrototypeOf(value);
	if (prototype !== Object.prototype && prototype !== null) return false;
	if (Reflect.ownKeys(value).some((key) => typeof key !== "string")) return false;
	if (ancestors.has(value)) return false;
	ancestors.add(value);
	try {
		for (const descriptor of Object.values(Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptors(value))) {
			if (!descriptor.enumerable || !("value" in descriptor) || !check(descriptor.value, ancestors)) {
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	} finally {
		ancestors.delete(value);
	}
}

// ══ delta/apply-immutable-trusted.ts ════════════════════════════════════
const DATA_DESCRIPTOR                     = {
	value: undefined,
	writable: true,
	enumerable: true,
	configurable: true,
};

/** Apply self-produced trusted operations while copying each touched container once. */
function applyImmutableTrusted   (target   , operations               )    {
	let root = target                        ;
	const owned = new WeakSet        ();
	for (const operation of operations) {
		if (operation[0] === "r") {
			root = operation[1];
			continue;
		}
		if (operation[0] === "p" || operation[0] === "m") {
			const copied = copyPath(root, operation[1], operation[1].length, owned);
			root = copied.root;
			if (!Array.isArray(copied.target)) throw new TypeError("Trusted array operation target is not an array");
			if (operation[0] === "p") spliceTrusted(copied.target, operation[2], operation[3], operation[4]);
			else permuteTrusted(copied.target, operation[2]);
			continue;
		}

		const path = operation[1];
		const copied = copyPath(root, path, path.length - 1, owned);
		root = copied.root;
		const key = path[path.length - 1] ;
		switch (operation[0]) {
			case "s":
				defineData(copied.target, key, operation[2]);
				break;
			case "d":
				if (Array.isArray(copied.target)) {
					if (typeof key !== "number") throw new TypeError("Trusted array deletion key is not numeric");
					spliceTrusted(copied.target, key, 1, []);
				} else Reflect.deleteProperty(copied.target, key);
				break;
			case "a": {
				const current = read(copied.target, key);
				if (typeof current !== "string") throw new TypeError("Trusted append target is not a string");
				defineData(copied.target, key, `${current}${operation[2]}`);
				break;
			}
			case "t": {
				const current = read(copied.target, key);
				if (typeof current !== "string") throw new TypeError("Trusted truncate target is not a string");
				defineData(copied.target, key, current.slice(operation[2]));
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	return root                ;
}

function copyPath(root           , path      , length        , owned                 )             {
	if (!trustedIsContainer(root)) throw new TypeError("Trusted operation root is not a container");
	let copiedRoot = root;
	if (!owned.has(root)) {
		copiedRoot = shallowCopy(root);
		owned.add(copiedRoot);
	}
	let destination = copiedRoot                 ;
	for (let index = 0; index < length; index++) {
		const segment = path[index] ;
		const child = read(destination, segment);
		if (!trustedIsContainer(child)) throw new TypeError("Trusted operation path is not a container");
		if (owned.has(child)) {
			destination = child;
			continue;
		}
		const copy = shallowCopy(child);
		defineData(destination, segment, copy);
		owned.add(copy);
		destination = copy;
	}
	return { root: copiedRoot, target: destination };
}

function shallowCopy(value               )                {
	if (Array.isArray(value)) return Array.from(value);
	const result = Object.create(Object.getPrototypeOf(value) === null ? null : Object.prototype)           
		       
		         
	 ;
	for (const key of Object.keys(value)) defineData(result, key, value[key] );
	return result;
}

function read(target               , key     )            {
	return (target                          )[key] ;
}

function defineData(target               , key     , value           )       {
	DATA_DESCRIPTOR.value = value;
	Object.defineProperty(target, key, DATA_DESCRIPTOR);
	DATA_DESCRIPTOR.value = undefined;
}

function spliceTrusted(target             , start        , remove        , items                      )       {
	const oldLength = target.length;
	const delta = items.length - remove;
	if (delta > 0) {
		target.length = oldLength + delta;
		target.copyWithin(start + items.length, start + remove, oldLength);
	} else if (delta < 0) {
		target.copyWithin(start + items.length, start + remove, oldLength);
		target.length = oldLength + delta;
	}
	for (let index = 0; index < items.length; index++) defineData(target, start + index, items[index] );
}

function permuteTrusted(target             , permutation                   )       {
	if (target.length !== permutation.length) throw new TypeError("Trusted permutation length mismatch");
	const previous = target.slice();
	for (let index = 0; index < permutation.length; index++) target[index] = previous[permutation[index] ] ;
}

function trustedIsContainer(value         )                         {
	return value !== null && typeof value === "object";
}

// ══ delta/tracker.ts ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// object, base-array entry, inserted-array entry

const NODE = Symbol("chord.delta.overlay.node");
const PREPARED = new WeakMap                        ();
const RELEASED                            = {};
const ARRAY_MUTATORS = new Set             ([
	"push",
	"pop",
	"shift",
	"unshift",
	"splice",
	"reverse",
	"sort",
	"fill",
	"copyWithin",
]);
const MAX_DELTA_OPERATIONS = 4_096;
const MAX_SIMPLE_OBJECT_NODES = 128;
const trackerDATA_DESCRIPTOR                     = {
	value: undefined,
	writable: true,
	enumerable: true,
	configurable: true,
};

                                             
	                 
	                  
	                            
	                              
	              
 

                                           
	                         
	                       
	              
 

                                            
	                  
	                          
	                         
	                                      
	                                   
 

class PreparedImpl                                          {
	         #context                ;
	         value   ;
	         base   ;
	         ops               ;

	constructor(context                , value   , operations               ) {
		this.#context = context;
		this.value = value;
		this.base = context.baseValue     ;
		this.ops = operations;
		PREPARED.set(this, context);
	}

	get baseRevision()         {
		return this.#context.baseRevision;
	}

	abort()       {
		abortContext(this.#context);
	}
}

class ChangeImpl                                        {
	#context                            ;
	#preparedStatus                        ;
	#settled = false;

	constructor(context                ) {
		this.#context = context;
	}

	get state()           {
		const state = this.#context?.root?.proxy;
		if (state === undefined) throw new TypeError("Cannot use a settled overlay");
		return state            ;
	}

	prepare()              {
		if (this.#settled) throw new Error("Change has already been settled");
		const context = this.#context ;
		assertWritable(context);
		context.status.value = "prepared";
		try {
			if (!context.replacement) context.ops = context.dirty.length === 0 ? [] : emitOperations(context);
			const prepared = materializePrepared   (context);
			this.#preparedStatus = context.status;
			this.#settled = true;
			return prepared;
		} catch (error) {
			context.status.value = "aborted";
			clearContext(context);
			this.#settled = true;
			throw error;
		} finally {
			this.#context = undefined;
		}
	}

	abort()       {
		if (this.#settled) {
			if (this.#preparedStatus?.value === "prepared") this.#preparedStatus.value = "aborted";
			this.#preparedStatus = undefined;
			return;
		}
		this.#settled = true;
		abortContext(this.#context );
		this.#context = undefined;
	}
}

class TrackerImpl                                         {
	         #owner = {};
	         #contexts = new Set                         ();
	         #selfRef                              ;
	#value   ;
	#revision = 0;
	#pruneBudget = 256;

	constructor(initial   ) {
		this.#value = initial;
		this.#selfRef = new WeakRef(this                                  );
	}

	get value()    {
		return this.#value;
	}

	get revision()         {
		return this.#revision;
	}

	beginChange()            {
		const context = createContext(this.#selfRef, this.#owner, this.#revision, this.#value, false, this.#value);
		this.#register(context);
		return new ChangeImpl(context);
	}

	prepareReplace(value   )              {
		const context = createContext(this.#selfRef, this.#owner, this.#revision, value, true, this.#value);
		context.status.value = "prepared";
		this.#register(context);
		try {
			return materializePrepared   (context);
		} catch (error) {
			context.status.value = "aborted";
			clearContext(context);
			throw error;
		}
	}

	adopt(prepared             )       {
		const context = PREPARED.get(prepared          );
		if (context?.owner !== this.#owner) throw new Error("Prepared change belongs to a different tracker");
		if (context.status.value === "consumed") throw new Error("Prepared change has already been used");
		if (context.status.value === "aborted") throw new Error("Prepared change has been aborted");
		if (context.status.value === "stale") throw new Error("Prepared change is stale");
		if (context.status.value !== "prepared") throw new Error("Prepared change is not ready");
		if (context.baseRevision !== this.#revision) {
			context.status.value = "stale";
			clearContext(context);
			throw new Error("Prepared change is stale");
		}

		if (this.#value !== prepared.base) {
			context.status.value = "stale";
			throw new Error("Prepared change is stale");
		}
		// Materialization happened during prepare. Adoption is an infallible pointer
		// swap so storage failure can discard the candidate without touching authority.
		this.#value = prepared.value;
		context.status.value = "consumed";
		this.#revision += 1;
		this.#invalidate(context);
	}

	releaseContext(context                )       {
		if (context.registryRef !== undefined) this.#contexts.delete(context.registryRef);
		context.registryRef = undefined;
	}

	#register(context                )       {
		const reference = new WeakRef(context);
		context.registryRef = reference;
		this.#contexts.add(reference);
		this.#pruneBudget -= 1;
		if (this.#pruneBudget === 0) {
			this.#prune();
			this.#pruneBudget = Math.max(256, this.#contexts.size);
		}
	}

	#prune()       {
		for (const reference of this.#contexts) {
			if (reference.deref() === undefined) this.#contexts.delete(reference);
		}
	}

	#invalidate(winner                )       {
		for (const reference of this.#contexts) {
			const context = reference.deref();
			if (context === undefined || context === winner) continue;
			if (context.status.value === "open" || context.status.value === "prepared") context.status.value = "stale";
			// Adoption must not walk every node touched by a competing draft. Drop the
			// context's strong overlay references in O(1); externally retained proxies
			// still see the stale status and are reclaimed with their holders.
			releaseOverlayReferences(context);
		}
		this.#contexts.clear();
		this.#pruneBudget = 256;
	}

	replacementBase(context                )                {
		return context.baseRevision === this.#revision ? this.#value : undefined;
	}
}

/** Take immutable ownership of an alias-free strict-JSON root in O(1). */
function track                  (initial   )             {
	return new TrackerImpl(initial);
}

function materializePrepared                  (context                )                  {
	const operations = ensureOperations(context);
	const base = context.baseValue     ;
	// Operation payloads are already detached from the mutable overlay. The
	// resulting revision and public batch intentionally share those payloads under
	// the trusted immutable-transfer contract.
	const value =
		operations.length === 0
			? base
			: context.simpleObjectMaterialization
				? (cloneNode(context.root )     )
				: materializeOperations(base, operations);
	const prepared = new PreparedImpl(context, value, operations);
	if (context.nodes.length > 4_096) releaseOverlayReferences(context);
	else clearContext(context);
	return prepared;
}

function materializeOperations                  (base   , operations               )    {
	for (const operation of operations) {
		// Native splice/permutation remains faster for large structural batches.
		if (operation[0] === "p" || operation[0] === "m") return applyImmutable(base, operations);
	}
	return applyImmutableTrusted(base, operations);
}

function createContext                  (
	tracker                              ,
	owner        ,
	baseRevision        ,
	root   ,
	replacement         ,
	base   ,
)                 {
	const context                 = {
		owner,
		tracker,
		baseRevision,
		status: { value: "open" },
		root: undefined,
		bases: [],
		stored: [],
		dirty: [],
		nodes: [],
		rawNodes: new WeakMap(),
		ops: undefined,
		replacement,
		replacementNoop: false,
		baseValue: base,
		overlayReleased: false,
		simpleObjectMaterialization: false,
		registryRef: undefined,
	};
	context.root = createNode(context, root             , undefined);
	return context;
}

const sharedObjectHandler                       = {
	deleteProperty(target, property) {
		return deleteProperty(nodeForTarget(target), property);
	},
	defineProperty(target) {
		assertWritable(nodeForTarget(target).context);
		throw new TypeError("Defining overlay properties is not supported");
	},
	get(target, property) {
		if (property === "then") {
			const node = (target                                )[NODE]                           ;
			if (node === undefined || isSettledContext(node.context)) return undefined;
		}
		const node = nodeForTarget(target);
		if (property === NODE) return node;
		return getProperty(node, property);
	},
	getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target, property) {
		return getDescriptor(nodeForTarget(target), property);
	},
	getPrototypeOf(target) {
		const node = nodeForTarget(target);
		assertReadable(node.context);
		return Object.getPrototypeOf(nodeBase(node));
	},
	has(target, property) {
		return hasProperty(nodeForTarget(target), property);
	},
	isExtensible(target) {
		assertReadable(nodeForTarget(target).context);
		return true;
	},
	ownKeys(target) {
		return ownKeys(nodeForTarget(target));
	},
	preventExtensions(target) {
		assertWritable(nodeForTarget(target).context);
		throw new TypeError("Overlays cannot be made non-extensible");
	},
	set(target, property, value) {
		return setProperty(nodeForTarget(target), property, value);
	},
	setPrototypeOf(target) {
		assertWritable(nodeForTarget(target).context);
		throw new TypeError("Changing an overlay prototype is not supported");
	},
};

const sharedArrayHandler                       = sharedObjectHandler;

function createNode(
	context                ,
	base           ,
	parent                         ,
	parentKind             = 0,
	parentKey                  = "",
	parentSource                           = undefined,
	parentPlacement = false,
)              {
	const existing = context.rawNodes .get(base);
	if (existing !== undefined) return existing;
	const target         = Array.isArray(base) ? [] : {};
	const baseIndex = context.bases .length;
	const parentIndex = parent?.baseIndex ?? -1;
	context.bases .push(base);
	const node              = {
		context,
		baseIndex,
		parentIndex,
		parentKind,
		parentKey,
		parentPlacement,
		target,
		proxy: target,
	};
	if (parentSource !== undefined) node.parentSource = parentSource;
	(target                                )[NODE] = node;
	node.proxy = new Proxy(target, Array.isArray(base) ? sharedArrayHandler : sharedObjectHandler);
	context.rawNodes .set(base, node);
	context.nodes.push(node);
	return node;
}

function nodeBase(node             )            {
	return node.context.bases [node.baseIndex] ;
}

function nodeParent(node             )                          {
	return node.parentIndex < 0 ? undefined : node.context.nodes[node.parentIndex];
}

function storeValue(context                , value        )            {
	if (!isContainer(value)) return value;
	const index = context.stored .length;
	context.stored .push(value);
	return { index };
}

function storedValue(context                , reference           )         {
	return typeof reference === "object" && reference !== null ? context.stored [reference.index]  : reference;
}

function replaceStoredValue(context                , reference           , value        )            {
	if (isContainer(value)) {
		if (typeof reference === "object" && reference !== null) {
			context.stored [reference.index] = value;
			return reference;
		}
		return storeValue(context, value);
	}
	if (typeof reference === "object" && reference !== null) context.stored [reference.index] = RELEASED;
	return value;
}

function releaseStoredValue(context                , reference           )       {
	if (typeof reference === "object" && reference !== null) context.stored [reference.index] = RELEASED;
}

function nodeForTarget(target        )              {
	const node = (target                                )[NODE]                           ;
	if (node === undefined) throw new TypeError("Cannot use a settled overlay");
	return node;
}

function isSettledContext(context                )          {
	return (
		context.overlayReleased ||
		context.status.value === "consumed" ||
		context.status.value === "aborted" ||
		context.status.value === "stale"
	);
}

function assertReadable(context                )       {
	if (isSettledContext(context)) throw new TypeError("Cannot use a settled overlay");
}

function assertWritable(context                )       {
	assertReadable(context);
	if (context.status.value !== "open") throw new TypeError("Prepared overlays are read-only");
}

function getProperty(node             , property             )          {
	assertReadable(node.context);
	if (Array.isArray(nodeBase(node))) {
		const overlay = arrayOverlay(node);
		if (property === "length") return arrayLength(overlay);
		if (ARRAY_MUTATORS.has(property)) return arrayMutators[property                              ];
		const index = arrayIndex(property);
		if (index !== undefined) return getArrayIndex(node, index);
		return Reflect.get(Array.prototype, property, node.proxy);
	}
	if (typeof property === "symbol") return Reflect.get(nodeBase(node), property, node.proxy);
	const key = String(property);
	if (!objectHas(node, key)) {
		if (isObjectDeleted(node, key) || Object.hasOwn(nodeBase(node), key)) return undefined;
		return Reflect.get(nodeBase(node), property, node.proxy);
	}
	const value = objectValue(node, key);
	if (!isContainer(value)) return value;
	return createNode(node.context, value, node, 0, key, undefined, hasObjectWrite(node, key)).proxy;
}

function getArrayIndex(node             , index        )          {
	const overlay = arrayOverlay(node);
	if (index >= arrayLength(overlay)) return undefined;
	const piece = locatePiece(overlay, index);
	const sourceIndex = piece.start + piece.step * overlay.locatedOffset;
	const value = entryValueAt(node, piece, sourceIndex);
	if (!isContainer(value)) return value;
	return createNode(
		node.context,
		value,
		node,
		piece.kind === "base" ? 1 : 2,
		sourceIndex,
		piece.kind === "insert" ? piece.source : undefined,
		piece.kind === "insert" || hasEntryOverrideAt(overlay, piece, sourceIndex),
	).proxy;
}

function setProperty(node             , property             , supplied         )          {
	assertWritable(node.context);
	if (typeof property === "symbol") throw new TypeError("Symbol writes are not supported");
	if (Array.isArray(nodeBase(node))) {
		if (property === "length") {
			setArrayLength(node, toArrayLength(supplied));
			return true;
		}
		const index = arrayIndex(property);
		if (index === undefined) throw new TypeError("Only array indices and length can be written");
		if (index > arrayLength(arrayOverlay(node))) throw new TypeError("Overlay arrays cannot contain holes");
		setArrayIndex(node, index, clonePlacement(supplied));
		return true;
	}
	const key = String(property);
	if (supplied === undefined) return deleteProperty(node, key);
	const stored = clonePlacement(supplied);
	const current = objectValue(node, key);
	const wasDeleted = isObjectDeleted(node, key);
	if (!wasDeleted && !isContainer(stored) && current === stored) return true;
	setObjectWrite(node, key, stored);
	if (wasDeleted && Object.hasOwn(nodeBase(node), key)) {
		if (node.readded === undefined) node.readded = new Set();
		node.readded.add(key);
	}
	deleteObjectDeletion(node, key);
	markDirty(node);
	return true;
}

function deleteProperty(node             , property             )          {
	assertWritable(node.context);
	if (typeof property === "symbol") throw new TypeError("Symbol writes are not supported");
	if (Array.isArray(nodeBase(node))) throw new TypeError("Overlay arrays cannot contain holes");
	const key = String(property);
	if (!objectHas(node, key)) return true;
	deleteObjectWrite(node, key);
	node.readded?.delete(key);
	setObjectDeletion(node, key);
	markDirty(node);
	return true;
}

function hasProperty(node             , property             )          {
	assertReadable(node.context);
	if (Array.isArray(nodeBase(node))) {
		if (property === "length") return true;
		const index = arrayIndex(property);
		if (index !== undefined) return index < arrayLength(arrayOverlay(node));
		return property in Array.prototype;
	}
