				this.host.facetApi(((type             , factory                      , options               = {}) =>
					this.host.serveInto(this.services, makeService(type, factory, options, facet.name)))         ),
			);
		}
		for (const [name, service] of this.services) if (current.get(name) !== service) this.changed.add(name);
		for (const name of current.keys()) if (!this.services.has(name)) this.removed.add(name);
		this.host.pending = {
			id: this.host.nextEpochId(this.epoch),
			services: this.services,
			tables: new Map(),
			roots: new Map(),
		};
	}

	async withDeadline   (promise            , what        )             {
		const deadline = this.options.gateDeadline ?? 5000;
		let timer                                           ;
		const timeout = new Promise       ((_, reject) => {
			timer = setTimeout(
				() => reject(new ChordError("aborted", `${what} within ${deadline} ms`, { reason: "deadline" })),
				deadline,
			);
		});
		try {
			return await Promise.race([promise, timeout]);
		} finally {
			clearTimeout(timer);
		}
	}

	/** Same contract version must cover the old description (forgotten bump); facets' `uses` must bind. */
	checkProviders()       {
		for (const name of this.changed) {
			const next = this.services.get(name) .contract;
			const old = this.host.current.services.get(name)?.contract;
			if (old === undefined || old.version !== next.version) continue;
			const reason = coverage(next.description, old.description);
			if (reason !== undefined) {
				throw new ChordError(
					"contract_mismatch",
					`${name}@${next.version} changed incompatibly without a version bump (${reason})`,
				);
			}
		}
		const facets = new Map(this.host.facets);
		for (const facet of this.facets) facets.set(facet.name, facet);
		for (const name of this.options.remove ?? []) facets.delete(name);
		for (const facet of facets.values()) {
			for (const token of facet.uses ?? []) {
				const service = this.services.get(token.name);
				if (service !== undefined && this.changed.has(token.name))
					bind(service.contract, service.options.accepts, token);
			}
		}
	}

	/**
	 * Prepares every live record of a changed type, including records that activate meanwhile, to a fixpoint. Records
	 * already running the new code (types this reload adds) are skipped.
	 */
	async prepareAll()                {
		for (;;) {
			const todo = [...this.host.records.values()].filter(
				(record) =>
					this.changed.has(record.typeName) &&
					!this.candidates.has(record) &&
					record.service !== this.services.get(record.typeName) &&
					(record.status === "live" || record.status === "activating"),
			);
			if (todo.length === 0) return;
			await Promise.allSettled(todo.map((record) => record.ready));
			this.#alive();
			for (const record of todo) {
				if (record.status === "live" && record.service !== this.services.get(record.typeName)) {
					await this.prepareRecord(record);
					this.#alive();
				}
			}
		}
	}

	/** Runs the new factory in recording mode and opens what can be opened without the gate. */
	async prepareRecord(record            )                {
		const service = this.services.get(record.typeName) ;
		const run = record.run(service, record.decls);
		const candidate            = {
			record,
			service,
			run,
			reasons: [],
			atGate: [],
			replaced: [],
			migrated: new Map(),
			derived: new Map(),
			staged: undefined,
		};
		this.candidates.set(record, candidate);
		const bumped = service.stateVersion !== record.stateVersion;
		if (bumped) candidate.reasons.push(`state version ${record.stateVersion} -> ${service.stateVersion}`);
		for (const [name, cell] of run.decls.cells) {
			if (record.decls.cells.get(name) !== cell || deepEqual(cell.schema, run.decls.cellSchemas.get(name))) continue;
			if (!bumped) {
				throw invalid("state", `${record.address}: schema of state ${name} changed without a state-version bump`);
			}
		}
		for (const name of Object.keys(service.options.state?.migrate ?? {})) {
			if (!run.decls.cells.has(name))
				throw invalid("state", `${record.address}: migrate for undeclared state ${name}`);
		}
		const deferred = new Set              ();
		for (const resource of run.created.resources) {
			if (resource.exclusive || resource.deps.some((dep) => deferred.has(dep                )))
				deferred.add(resource);
		}
		for (const resource of deferred) {
			candidate.atGate.push(resource);
			const old = record.decls.resources.get(resource.key);
			if (resource.exclusive && old?.exclusive && old.state === "open") candidate.replaced.push(old);
		}
		if (deferred.size > 0) candidate.reasons.push("exclusive resource replaced");
		if (candidate.reasons.length === 0) dryRun(candidate);
		await record.open(
			run.created.resources.filter((resource) => !deferred.has(resource)),
			run.created.holds,
			this.abort.signal,
		);
	}

	/**
	 * Every hold that survives into the new epoch must bind to the new provider. Browser holds don't block a reload:
	 * after the commit they get `upgrade_required` instead.
	 */
	checkHolds()       {
		const dropped = new Set         ();
		for (const { record, run } of this.candidates.values()) {
			for (const hold of record.decls.holds.values())
				if (![...run.decls.holds.values()].includes(hold)) dropped.add(hold);
		}
		for (const record of this.host.records.values()) {
			const service = this.services.get(record.typeName);
			if (service === undefined || !this.changed.has(record.typeName)) continue;
			for (const hold of record.holds) {
				// The host's bootstrap hold follows this provider at commit; it is not an old-code consumer.
				if (hold === this.host.eager.get(record.typeName)) continue;
				if (!dropped.has(hold) && hold.owner !== "client")
					bind(service.contract, service.options.accepts, hold.contract);
			}
		}
	}

	entries()                               {
		return this.host
			.liveDecls(true)
			.filter(([record]) => !this.removed.has(record.typeName))
			.map(([record, decls]) => [record, this.candidates.get(record)?.run.decls ?? decls]);
	}

	/**
	 * Admission cut, wait for grandfathered roots (deadline), replace exclusive resources, migrate into private
	 * copies. Old tasks of gated records are stopped at the cut; listener events are queued until commit or abort.
	 */
	async gate()                {
		this.gated = [...this.candidates.values()].filter(
			(candidate) => candidate.reasons.length > 0 && candidate.record.status === "live",
		);
		const drained = this.gated.map(({ record }) => {
			const grandfathered = new Set(record.leases.keys());
			return new Promise      ((resolve) => {
				record.gate = { grandfathered, queue: [], drained: resolve };
				record.stopTasks();
				if (grandfathered.size === 0) resolve();
			});
		});
		const deadline = this.options.gateDeadline ?? 5000;
		let timer                                           ;
		const timedOut = await Promise.race([
			Promise.all(drained).then(() => false),
			new Promise         ((resolve) => {
				timer = setTimeout(() => resolve(true), deadline);
			}),
		]);
		clearTimeout(timer);
		if (timedOut) {
			const blocking = this.gated.flatMap(({ record }) => [...(record.gate?.grandfathered ?? [])]);
			throw new ChordError(
				"aborted",
				`gate deadline (${deadline} ms) missed; blocking roots: ${blocking.join(", ")}`,
				{
					reason: "gate_deadline",
					details: { blocking },
				},
			);
		}
		this.#alive();
		for (const candidate of this.gated) {
			const { record } = candidate;
			for (const old of candidate.replaced) {
				// Owned by the rollback before closing: a failing close must still be restored.
				this.closed.push([record, old]);
				await old.close(record.openOrder);
			}
			await record.open(candidate.atGate, [], this.abort.signal);
			this.#alive();
		}
		for (const candidate of this.gated) this.migrate(candidate);
	}

	migrate(candidate           )       {
		const { record, service, run } = candidate;
		const migrate = service.options.state?.migrate ?? {};
		const bumped = service.stateVersion !== record.stateVersion;
		for (const [name, cell] of run.decls.cells) {
			if (record.decls.cells.get(name) !== cell) continue;
			const schema = run.decls.cellSchemas.get(name) ;
			const fn = migrate[name];
			let value         ;
			if (bumped && fn !== undefined) value = fn(structuredClone(cell.value), record.stateVersion);
			else if (!deepEqual(cell.schema, schema)) value = cell.value;
			else continue;
			assertValid("state", `${record.address}: migrated state ${name}`, schema, value);
			candidate.migrated.set(cell, value);
		}
		dryRun(candidate);
	}

	async rollback()                {
		if (this.rolledBack) return;
		this.rolledBack = true;
		this.abort.abort(new ChordError("aborted", "reload aborted"));
		const report = (error         )       => this.host.report(error);
		for (const candidate of this.candidates.values()) candidate.staged?.discard();
		for (const staged of this.deferred.values()) staged.discard();
		for (const { record, run } of this.candidates.values()) {
			record.pending.delete(run.decls);
			await record.unwind(run).catch(report);
		}
		// Replaced exclusive resources are restored; one that cannot be is marked unavailable, never silently closed.
		for (const [record, resource] of this.closed) {
			if (resource.state !== "closed") continue;
			await resource.open(new AbortController().signal, record.openOrder).catch((error         ) => {
				resource.markUnavailable();
				report(
					new ChordError("unavailable", `${record.address}: resource ${resource.key} could not be restored`, {
						cause: error,
					}),
				);
			});
		}
		for (const { record } of this.gated) {
			if (record.gate === undefined) continue;
			record.openGate();
			for (const name of record.decls.tasks.keys()) record.startTask(name, this.host.current.id);
		}
		// Records of types this reload would have added lose their code.
		const reason = new ChordError("gone", "reload aborted; type not served");
		for (const record of [...this.host.records.values()]) {
			if (!this.host.current.services.has(record.typeName)) await record.dispose(true, reason).catch(report);
		}
		this.host.pending = undefined;
		this.host.gating = undefined;
	}

	/**
	 * Publishes the new epoch in one synchronous step. Everything fallible ran in prepare (factories, opens, migration
	 * and derivation checks against the prospective schemas, listener subscriptions), so this only installs. Observers
	 * (state, shapes, registries, listener roots) are notified after the whole step. If an install still throws, the
	 * epoch is half-published: the host fails terminally and admits no more work.
	 */
	commit(beforeStart             )               {
		try {
			return holdPublications(() => {
				const report = this.#publish();
				// Installed, not yet started: the coordinator queues its commit frames here, so nothing this epoch starts
				// (tasks, eager activations, then the held listener/registry/state notifications) can reach a child first.
				beforeStart?.();
				for (const start of this.#starts.splice(0)) start();
				return report;
			});
		} catch (error) {
			this.host.fail(error);
			throw reloadError(error);
		}
	}

	/** Work that may start new-epoch roots, run after installation (and after the coordinator's commit frames). */
	         #starts                 = [];

	#publish()               {
		const host = this.host;
		const previous = host.current;
		const next             = host.pending ;
		host.pending = undefined;
		host.epochs.set(next.id, next);
		host.previous = previous;
		host.current = next;
		const prepared           = [];
		for (const record of [...host.records.values()]) {
			if (this.removed.has(record.typeName)) continue;
			const candidate = this.candidates.get(record);
			if (candidate === undefined) {
				const installed = record.behaviors.get(previous.id);
				if (installed !== undefined) record.behaviors.set(next.id, { ...installed, epoch: next.id });
				continue;
			}
			if (record.status !== "live") continue;
			this.install(candidate, next.id);
			prepared.push(record.address);
		}
		for (const { record, run, staged } of this.candidates.values()) {
			if (record.status === "live") continue;
			staged?.discard();
			record.pending.delete(run.decls);
			record.unwind(run).catch((error         ) => host.report(error));
		}
		// Records of types this reload adds start their runtime now.
		for (const [record, staged] of this.deferred) {
			if (record.status !== "live") {
				staged.discard();
				continue;
			}
			record.deferredRuntime = false;
			staged.activate();
			this.#starts.push(() => record.startTasks());
		}
		host.installTables(next, this.tables, true);
		const reason = new ChordError("gone", "type no longer served");
		for (const record of [...host.records.values()]) {
			if (this.removed.has(record.typeName))
				record.dispose(true, reason).catch((error         ) => host.report(error));
		}
		for (const name of this.removed) host.eager.delete(name);
		for (const facet of this.facets) host.facets.set(facet.name, facet);
		for (const name of this.options.remove ?? []) host.facets.delete(name);
		host.gating = undefined;
		for (const [name, service] of this.services) {
			if (service.options.eager && this.changed.has(name) && host.eager.get(name)?.record?.status !== "live") {
				this.#starts.push(() => host.startEager(service));
			}
		}
		return {
			epoch: next.id,
			previous: previous.id,
			changed: [...this.changed],
			removed: [...this.removed],
			prepared,
			gated: this.gated.map(({ record }) => record.address),
		};
	}

	install(candidate           , epoch        )       {
		const { record, run, service } = candidate;
		const gated = candidate.reasons.length > 0;
		record.pending.delete(run.decls);
		record.stopTasks();
		if (gated) {
			// Old derivations would compute over migrated values; the old behavior can no longer run anyway.
			const kept = new Set(run.decls.derives.values());
			for (const derived of record.decls.derives.values()) if (!kept.has(derived)) derived.detach();
		}
		record.decls = run.decls;
		record.service = service;
		record.stateVersion = service.stateVersion;
		record.behaviors.set(epoch, { epoch, service, behavior: run.behavior, decls: run.decls });
		if (gated) {
			// Old code can never run over migrated state: drop the previous behavior now, not at retirement.
			record.minEpoch = epoch;
			for (const old of [...record.behaviors.keys()]) if (old !== epoch) this.host.retireBehavior(record, old);
		}
		// Schemas first, then the values validated against them in prepare.
		record.refreshSchemas();
		for (const [name, derived] of run.decls.derives) derived.setFn(run.decls.deriveFns.get(name) );
		for (const [name, cell] of run.decls.cells) {
			const schema = run.decls.cellSchemas.get(name) ;
			if (candidate.migrated.has(cell)) cell.reset(candidate.migrated.get(cell), schema);
			else cell.schema = schema;
		}
		const created = new Set(run.created.derives);
		for (const derived of run.created.derives) derived.attach(candidate.derived);
		for (const derived of run.decls.derives.values()) if (!created.has(derived)) derived.refresh();
		candidate.staged?.activate();
		this.#starts.push(() => record.startTasks());
		record.openGate();
		const eager = this.host.eager.get(record.typeName);
		if (eager?.record === record && eager.contract.fingerprint !== service.contract.fingerprint) {
			const replacement = new HandleImpl(record, service.contract, "host");
			// Acquire first: releasing the only hold must not dispose/reopen the surviving eager record.
			record.addHold(replacement);
			this.host.eager.set(record.typeName, replacement);
			eager.release();
		}
		for (const handle of [...record.holds]) {
			// Browser holds whose contract no longer binds are told to reload their code (they never block a reload).
			if (handle.owner === "client") {
				try {
					bind(service.contract, service.options.accepts, handle.contract);
				} catch (error) {
					handle.gone(
						new ChordError("upgrade_required", `${record.typeName} changed; reload the web facets`, {
							cause: error,
						}),
					);
					record.release(handle);
					continue;
				}
			}
			handle.shapeChanged();
		}
	}
}

// ══ wire.ts ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// JSON frames between two hosts (or a host and a client). Ids are chosen by the side that creates the hold, call or
// subscription; every frame type has one direction, so ids never collide:
//   consumer → provider: hold, release, call, cancel, sub, unsub
//   provider → consumer: shape, gone, result, state
//   both: hello, ping, pong, bye, root_end, ack; parent → child: invalidate, prepare, commit, abort, retire;
//   child → parent: vote
//
// Each frame is declared once, as the schema every received frame is checked against (`isFrame`); the types are derived
// from it. Payloads (hold inputs, call args, results, snapshots) stay `unknown`: they are validated where they are used,
// against the contract. State ops are checked op by op by the delta decoder.

const Id = Type.Integer({ minimum: 1 });
const Name = Type.String({ minLength: 1 });
const Reload = Type.Integer({ minimum: 1 });
/** Arrays a sender may pass as readonly. */
const List =                    (item   ) => Type.Immutable(Type.Array(item));

const RootRefSchema = Type.Object({ rootId: Name, epoch: Type.Integer({ minimum: 1 }) });
                                                   

const WireErrorSchema = Type.Object({
	code: Type.Enum(CHORD_ERROR_CODES),
	message: Type.String(),
	reason: Type.Optional(Type.String()),
});
                                                       

const WireReloadOptionsSchema = Type.Object({
	remove: Type.Optional(List(Type.String())),
	gateDeadline: Type.Optional(Type.Number()),
	retireDeadline: Type.Optional(Type.Number()),
});
                                                                       

/** A child's prepared reload, sent with its vote (same fields as `ReloadReport`, without children). */
const WireReloadReportSchema = Type.Object({
	epoch: Type.Integer(),
	previous: Type.Integer(),
	changed: List(Type.String()),
	removed: List(Type.String()),
	prepared: List(Type.String()),
	gated: List(Type.String()),
});
                                                                     

const FrameSchema = Type.Union([
	Type.Object({
		t: Type.Literal("hello"),
		name: Name,
		kind: Type.Union([Type.Literal("host"), Type.Literal("client")]),
		epoch: Type.Integer({ minimum: 0 }),
		/** The sender acknowledges what it receives (`ack`). Flow control applies only when both hellos say so. */
		acks: Type.Optional(Type.Boolean()),
	}),
	/** Every frame received so far except `ack` frames, counted from the link's start, once processed. */
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("ack"), n: Type.Integer({ minimum: 1 }) }),
	Type.Object({
		t: Type.Literal("hold"),
		id: Id,
		/** Target host name. */
		at: Name,
		/** The consumer's contract token; the provider checks that it covers it. */
		token: DescriptionSchema,
		input: Type.Unknown(),
		/** A forwarded principal. Honored only on frames from the parent link. */
		principal: Type.Optional(Type.Unknown()),
		/** Set by a relay when the hold originates from a client (browser): not a reload participant. */
		client: Type.Optional(Type.Boolean()),
	}),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("release"), id: Id }),
	Type.Object({
		t: Type.Literal("shape"),
		id: Id,
		kind: Type.Optional(Type.String()),
		members: List(Type.String()),
		contract: Type.String(),
		epoch: Type.Integer(),
	}),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("gone"), id: Id, error: WireErrorSchema }),
	Type.Object({
		t: Type.Literal("call"),
		id: Id,
		hold: Id,
		member: Name,
		args: Type.Unknown(),
		/** Absent: the receiving host starts a root (browsers never carry roots). */
		root: Type.Optional(RootRefSchema),
	}),
	Type.Object({
		t: Type.Literal("result"),
		id: Id,
		value: Type.Optional(Type.Unknown()),
		error: Type.Optional(WireErrorSchema),
	}),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("cancel"), id: Id }),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("sub"), id: Id, hold: Id, member: Name }),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("unsub"), id: Id }),
	Type.Object({
		t: Type.Literal("state"),
		id: Id,
		snap: Type.Optional(Type.Unknown()),
		reset: Type.Optional(Type.Unknown()),
		/** Encoded with the subscription's delta encoder; the decoder validates each op. */
		ops: Type.Optional(List(List(Type.Unknown()))),
		error: Type.Optional(WireErrorSchema),
	}),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("ping"), n: Type.Integer() }),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("pong"), n: Type.Integer() }),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("bye"), reason: Type.Optional(Type.String()) }),
	/** The root ended at its origin: its leases on records end (sent on every link the root's calls used). */
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("root_end"), rootId: Name }),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("invalidate"), pattern: Type.Record(Type.String(), Type.Unknown()) }),
	Type.Object({
		t: Type.Literal("prepare"),
		reload: Reload,
		epoch: Type.Integer({ minimum: 1 }),
		plugins: List(Type.String()),
		options: WireReloadOptionsSchema,
	}),
	Type.Object({
		t: Type.Literal("vote"),
		reload: Reload,
		error: Type.Optional(WireErrorSchema),
		report: Type.Optional(WireReloadReportSchema),
	}),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("commit"), reload: Reload }),
	/** The parent dropped `epoch`: roots of it may still come from there no longer. */
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("retire"), epoch: Type.Integer({ minimum: 1 }) }),
	Type.Object({ t: Type.Literal("abort"), reload: Reload }),
]);
                                               

/** A frame that passes validation; anything else is a protocol violation. */
const isFrame = (value         )                 => Value.Check(FrameSchema, value);

function toWireError(error         )            {
	if (error instanceof ChordError) {
		return error.reason === undefined
			? { code: error.code, message: error.message }
			: { code: error.code, message: error.message, reason: error.reason };
	}
	return { code: "unavailable", message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };
}

const fromWireError = (error           )             =>
	new ChordError(error.code, error.message.replace(/^[a-z_]+(\/[a-z_]+)?: /, ""), { reason: error.reason });

// ══ cluster.ts ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Cross-host reload, coordinated by the server (the host without a parent):
//   server: prepare itself and send `prepare` to every synced child host; collect votes
//   all ok: the server installs its new epoch, sends `commit` to every child, and only then starts what may make
//           new-epoch calls (tasks, listener and registry callbacks). Links are FIFO, so a child commits before it
//           can receive any frame of a root started in the new epoch.
//   any failure or missed vote deadline: `abort` everywhere; each host rolls back to where it was.
// Clients (browsers) are not participants: they see shape/reset pushes, or `upgrade_required`.
// A child that connects behind the server's epoch is fenced and reconciled with the same protocol.

let reloadSeq = 0;

const modulesFor = (remote                         , hostName                    )           => {
	let best                                         ;
	for (const entry of Object.entries(remote ?? {})) {
		if (hostName?.startsWith(entry[0]) && (best === undefined || entry[0].length > best[0].length)) best = entry;
	}
	return [...(best?.[1] ?? [])];
};

/** A vote, or its rejection after `deadline` milliseconds. */
function awaitVote(connection            , id        , deadline        )                   {
	let timer                                           ;
	const vote = new Promise         ((resolve, reject) => {
		connection.votes.set(id, { resolve, reject });
		timer = setTimeout(() => {
			connection.votes.delete(id);
			reject(new ChordError("aborted", `votes missing after ${deadline} ms`, { reason: "vote_deadline" }));
		}, deadline);
	});
	return vote.finally(() => clearTimeout(timer));
}

async function runClusterReload(
	host          ,
	plugins                      ,
	options               ,
)                        {
	// Fenced children (behind, being reconciled) are not participants; they reconcile to the new epoch afterwards.
	const participants = [...host.connections].filter(
		(connection) =>
			connection.role === "downstream" && connection.peer.kind === "host" && connection.open && connection.synced,
	);
	const epoch = host.peekNextEpoch();
	const reload = await beginReload(host, plugins, options, epoch);
	const id = ++reloadSeq;
	const deadline = options.voteDeadline ?? (options.gateDeadline ?? 5000) + (options.retireDeadline ?? 5000) + 5000;
	const votes = participants.map((connection) => {
		const name = connection.name ?? "child";
		const vote = awaitVote(connection, id, deadline);
		connection.send({
			t: "prepare",
			reload: id,
			epoch,
			plugins: modulesFor(options.remote, name),
			options: {
				remove: modulesFor(options.remoteRemove, name),
				gateDeadline: options.gateDeadline,
				retireDeadline: options.retireDeadline,
			},
		});
		return vote.then(
			(report) => [name, report                            ]         ,
			(error         ) => {
				const cause = reloadError(error);
				throw new ChordError(cause.code, `${name}: ${cause.message}`, { cause, reason: cause.reason });
			},
		);
	});
	let local = false;
	try {
		const results = await Promise.allSettled([
			reload.prepare().then(() => {
				local = true;
			}),
			...votes,
		]);
		const failed = results.find((result) => result.status === "rejected");
		if (failed !== undefined) {
			for (const connection of participants) {
				connection.votes.delete(id);
				connection.send({ t: "abort", reload: id });
			}
			if (local) await reload.rollback();
			throw failed.reason;
		}
		const report = reload.commit(() => {
			for (const connection of participants) {
				connection.send({ t: "commit", reload: id });
				connection.peerEpoch = epoch;
			}
		});
		host.rememberRemoteModules(options.remote, options.remoteRemove);
		const children                               = {};
		for (const result of results.slice(1)) {
			if (result.status !== "fulfilled") continue;
			const [name, child] = result.value                                               ;
			if (child !== undefined) children[name] = child;
		}
		return { ...report, children };
	} finally {
		endReload(host);
	}
}

/**
 * Brings a child that connected behind this host's epoch up to it: a reload of the child's modules (the ones
 * cluster reloads gave its name) to this host's current epoch, prepared, voted and committed like a cluster reload.
 * Until then the child is fenced: nothing is routed to it. The child's old code is never relabeled as a newer epoch.
 */
async function reconcileChild(host          , connection            )                {
	for (;;) {
		const epoch = host.current.id;
		if (!connection.open) return;
		if (connection.peerEpoch === epoch) {
			connection.markSynced(epoch);
			return;
		}
		const id = ++reloadSeq;
		const vote = awaitVote(connection, id, 30_000);
		connection.send({
			t: "prepare",
			reload: id,
			epoch,
			plugins: modulesFor(host.remoteModules, connection.name),
			options: { remove: modulesFor(host.remoteRemoved, connection.name) },
		});
		await vote;
		if (host.current.id !== epoch) {
			// This host moved on meanwhile: abort and try again with the newer epoch.
			connection.send({ t: "abort", reload: id });
			continue;
		}
		connection.send({ t: "commit", reload: id });
		connection.peerEpoch = epoch;
	}
}

/** One prepare the child received, registered before its first await so an abort or disconnect is never lost. */
                          
	                    
	                           
	                                            
	                   
 

/**
 * The child side: prepare and vote, then wait for the server's commit or abort. An abort or a lost parent link can
 * arrive at any point: while loading modules, while preparing (pending opens close themselves when they complete),
 * or after the vote. The operation is unwound once its pending work settles, and the reload slot is freed.
 */
class ChildReloads {
	         #host          ;
	#operation                            ;

	constructor(host          ) {
		this.#host = host;
	}

	handle(connection            , frame                                                       )       {
		if (frame.t === "prepare") {
			if (this.#operation !== undefined) {
				const error = new ChordError("unavailable", "a reload is already running", { reason: "reload_running" });
				connection.send({ t: "vote", reload: frame.reload, error: toWireError(error) });
				return;
			}
			const operation                 = { id: frame.reload, reload: undefined, state: "loading", cancelled: false };
			this.#operation = operation;
			this.#run(operation, connection, frame).catch((error         ) => this.#host.report(error));
			return;
		}
		const operation = this.#operation;
		if (operation === undefined || operation.id !== frame.reload) return;
		if (frame.t === "abort") {
			this.#cancel(operation);
			return;
		}
		if (operation.state !== "prepared") return;
		this.#operation = undefined;
		try {
			operation.reload?.commit();
		} finally {
			endReload(this.#host);
		}
	}

	/** The parent link died: nobody will commit, so roll back (now, or once pending work settles). */
	parentLost()       {
		if (this.#operation !== undefined) this.#cancel(this.#operation);
	}

	#cancel(operation                )       {
		operation.cancelled = true;
		if (operation.state !== "prepared") {
			// Loading or preparing: make prepare fail now; #run unwinds when it settles.
			operation.reload?.rollback().catch((error         ) => this.#host.report(error));
			return;
		}
		this.#operation = undefined;
		operation.reload
			?.rollback()
			.catch((error         ) => this.#host.report(error))
			.finally(() => endReload(this.#host));
	}

	async #run(
		operation                ,
		connection            ,
		frame                                  ,
	)                {
		const cancelled = ()             =>
			new ChordError("aborted", "reload aborted by the server", { reason: "aborted" });
		try {
			operation.reload = await beginReload(this.#host, frame.plugins, frame.options, frame.epoch);
			if (operation.cancelled) throw cancelled();
			operation.state = "preparing";
			await operation.reload.prepare();
			if (operation.cancelled) throw cancelled();
			operation.state = "prepared";
			connection.send({ t: "vote", reload: frame.reload, report: operation.reload.summary() });
		} catch (error) {
			if (operation.reload !== undefined) {
				await operation.reload.rollback().catch((cleanup         ) => this.#host.report(cleanup));
				endReload(this.#host);
			}
			if (this.#operation === operation) this.#operation = undefined;
			if (!operation.cancelled) connection.send({ t: "vote", reload: frame.reload, error: toWireError(error) });
		}
	}
}

// ══ link.ts ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
/** An ordered (FIFO), bidirectional frame channel between two hosts. */
                       
	                         
	   
	                                                                                                                    
	                                                                                                                     
	                                 
	   
	                                       
	                                                                    
	                                               
	                                                 
	                                               
	                             
 

/** Shared plumbing: receive queue, close handlers, `bye`. Transports implement `transmit` and `shutdown`. */
         class BaseLink                 {
	#handler                                      ;
	#queue          = [];
	         #closeHandlers = new Set                          ();
	#closed = false;
	/** The tail of frames being decoded asynchronously; undefined when none is. */
	#decoding                           ;

	get closed()          {
		return this.#closed;
	}

	                                                              
	                                    

	send(frame       )                     {
		if (this.#closed) return undefined;
		return this.transmit(frame);
	}

	receive(handler                        )       {
		this.#handler = handler;
		for (const frame of this.#queue.splice(0)) handler(frame);
	}

	onClose(handler                          )       {
		if (this.#closed) handler("closed");
		else this.#closeHandlers.add(handler);
	}

	close(reason = "closed")       {
		if (this.#closed) return;
		try {
			this.transmit({ t: "bye", reason });
		} catch {
			// The transport may already be gone.
		}
		this.ended(reason);
	}

	/** Delivers a parsed frame from the transport, after any frame still being decoded (`deliverLater`). */
	          deliver(frame       )       {
		if (this.#decoding !== undefined) this.deliverLater(Promise.resolve(frame));
		else this.#deliverNow(frame);
	}

	/** Delivers a frame that is still being decoded (inflated in a browser); later frames wait for it, in order. */
	          deliverLater(frame                )       {
		const next = (this.#decoding ?? Promise.resolve())
			.then(() => frame)
			.then(
				(decoded) => this.#deliverNow(decoded),
				() => this.close("protocol error"),
			)
			.finally(() => {
				if (this.#decoding === next) this.#decoding = undefined;
			});
		this.#decoding = next;
	}

	#deliverNow(frame       )       {
		if (this.#closed) return;
		if (frame.t === "bye") {
			this.ended(frame.reason ?? "peer closed");
			return;
		}
		if (this.#handler === undefined) this.#queue.push(frame);
		else this.#handler(frame);
	}

	/** Marks the link closed (transport ended) and notifies once. */
	          ended(reason        )       {
		if (this.#closed) return;
		this.#closed = true;
		this.#queue = [];
		try {
			this.shutdown();
		} finally {
			for (const handler of this.#closeHandlers) handler(reason);
			this.#closeHandlers.clear();
		}
	}
}

// ─── payload packing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//
// A transport optimization, invisible above the links. A frame's payload (call args, a result value, a state snapshot)
// whose JSON exceeds `PACK_THRESHOLD` characters is sent deflated. A host that only relays the frame forwards the
// deflated bytes unread (`Packed`); the host that uses the payload inflates it (`unpack`). Only hosts with Node's zlib
// deflate (synchronously, so frames keep their order); a browser inflates with `DecompressionStream` before delivery.

/** Payloads whose JSON is longer than this many characters are deflated. */
const PACK_THRESHOLD = 64 * 1024;

// pir: bind the header's node:zlib import into the same shape upstream's getBuiltinModule lookup produced.
const zlib = { deflateRawSync, inflateRawSync };

/** A deflated JSON payload, kept as received until a host uses it. */
class Packed {
	         bytes            ;
	constructor(bytes            ) {
		this.bytes = bytes;
	}
}

/** A frame payload as its user needs it: inflated and parsed if it arrived packed. */
function unpack(value         )          {
	if (!(value instanceof Packed)) return value;
	if (zlib === undefined) throw new TypeError("packed payload without zlib");
	return JSON.parse(zlib.inflateRawSync(value.bytes).toString("utf8"));
}

                                                        
const PAYLOAD_FIELDS                      = new Set              (["args", "value", "snap", "reset"]);

function payloadField(frame       )                           {
	if (frame.t === "call") return "args";
	if (frame.t === "result") return frame.value === undefined ? undefined : "value";
	if (frame.t === "state") return frame.snap !== undefined ? "snap" : frame.reset !== undefined ? "reset" : undefined;
	return undefined;
}

/** The frame without its payload field (the envelope). */
function envelopeOf(frame       , field              )                          {
	const envelope                          = { ...frame };
	delete envelope[field];
	return envelope;
}

/** The payload's deflated bytes: as received, or deflated now if large. Undefined: send it as JSON. */
function packedBytes(value         , compress         , json         )                         {
	if (value instanceof Packed) return value.bytes;
	if (!compress || zlib === undefined || json === undefined || json.length <= PACK_THRESHOLD) return undefined;
	return zlib.deflateRawSync(json, { level: 1 });
}

/** For object transports (Node IPC, memory): a packed payload travels as `z: [field, base64]`. */
function packObject(frame       , compress         )          {
	const field = payloadField(frame);
	if (field === undefined) return frame;
	const value = (frame                           )[field];
	const bytes = packedBytes(value, compress, compress && zlib !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(value) : undefined);
	if (bytes === undefined) return frame;
	return {
		...envelopeOf(frame, field),
		z: [field, Buffer.from(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength).toString("base64")],
	};
}

function unpackObject(message         )          {
	if (message === null || typeof message !== "object" || !("z" in message)) return message;
	const { z, ...rest } = message                                            ;
	if (!Array.isArray(z) || !PAYLOAD_FIELDS.has(z[0]) || typeof z[1] !== "string")
		throw new TypeError("bad packed payload");
	return { ...rest, [z[0]]: new Packed(Buffer.from(z[1], "base64")) };
}

const linkEncoder = new TextEncoder();
const linkDecoder = new TextDecoder();

/**
 * For WebSockets: a frame with a packed payload is a binary message, `[envelope length: u32 BE][envelope JSON, with
 * z: field][deflated payload]`; any other frame is text. The payload's JSON is spliced in, not encoded twice.
 */
function packMessage(frame       , compress         )                      {
	const field = payloadField(frame);
	if (field === undefined) return JSON.stringify(frame);
	const value = (frame                           )[field];
	const json = value instanceof Packed ? undefined : JSON.stringify(value);
	const bytes = packedBytes(value, compress, json);
	const envelope = envelopeOf(frame, field);
	if (bytes === undefined) {
		const head = JSON.stringify(envelope);
		return `${head.slice(0, -1)},${JSON.stringify(field)}:${json}}`;
	}
	const head = linkEncoder.encode(JSON.stringify({ ...envelope, z: field }));
	const message = new Uint8Array(4 + head.byteLength + bytes.byteLength);
	new DataView(message.buffer).setUint32(0, head.byteLength);
	message.set(head, 4);
	message.set(bytes, 4 + head.byteLength);
	return message;
}

/** The envelope of a binary message and its payload bytes. */
function splitMessage(data            )                                                                                {
	const length = new DataView(data.buffer, data.byteOffset, data.byteLength).getUint32(0);
	const envelope = JSON.parse(linkDecoder.decode(data.subarray(4, 4 + length)))           ;
	if (envelope === null || typeof envelope !== "object") throw new TypeError("bad envelope");
	const { z: field, ...rest } = envelope                                             ;
	if (typeof field !== "string" || !PAYLOAD_FIELDS.has(field)) throw new TypeError("bad packed payload");
	return { envelope: rest, field: field                , bytes: data.slice(4 + length) };
}

/**
 * The text frame of a binary (packed) WebSocket message, inflated: for a transport that relays text frames only (the
 * TUI launcher feeding its sandbox). Needs zlib.
 */
function inflateMessage(data            )         {
	if (zlib === undefined) throw new TypeError("inflateMessage needs zlib");
	const { envelope, field, bytes } = splitMessage(data);
	const head = JSON.stringify(envelope);
	return `${head.slice(0, -1)},${JSON.stringify(field)}:${zlib.inflateRawSync(bytes).toString("utf8")}}`;
}

/** Inflates without zlib (browsers). */
async function inflate(bytes            )                   {
	const stream = new Blob([bytes                           ])
		.stream()
		.pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream("deflate-raw"));
	return JSON.parse(await new Response(stream).text());
}

/** Parses a JSON frame; anything else is a protocol error. */
function parseFrame(data        )        {
	const frame = unpackObject(JSON.parse(data))         ;
	if (frame === null || typeof frame !== "object" || typeof frame.t !== "string") throw new TypeError("not a frame");
	return frame;
}

// ─── memory ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class MemoryLink extends BaseLink {
	peer                        ;
	#severed = false;
	#pending = Promise.resolve();

	/** Simulates a dead network: frames are dropped silently in both directions and no close is signalled. */
	sever()       {
		this.#severed = true;
		if (this.peer) this.peer.#severed = true;
	}

	          transmit(frame       )                     {
		if (this.#severed) return undefined;
		// A JSON round trip detaches the frame (and rejects what JSON would mangle silently, e.g. bigint).
		const data = JSON.stringify(packObject(frame, true));
		const peer = this.peer;
		// One promise chain per direction keeps delivery FIFO and asynchronous, like a real transport.
		this.#pending = this.#pending.then(() => {
			if (!this.#severed && peer !== undefined) peer.deliver(parseFrame(data));
		});
		return data.length;
	}

	          shutdown()       {
		const peer = this.peer;
		this.#pending = this.#pending.then(() => peer?.ended("peer closed"));
	}
}

/** Two connected in-memory links (tests). */
function memoryLinkPair()                           {
	const a = new MemoryLink();
	const b = new MemoryLink();
	a.peer = b;
	b.peer = a;
	return [a, b];
}

// ─── Node IPC ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** `process` in a forked child, or the parent's `ChildProcess`. Structural, so Chord needs no Node types here. */
                             
	                            
	                                
	                                                                    
	                                                       
	                                                                     
	                                                        
 

/**
 * Chord frames on an IPC channel are wrapped as `{ chord2: frame }`; other messages (control) pass by. Both ends are
 * Node processes of one application, so large payloads are packed both ways. Node serializes the messages itself, so
 * the link reports no sizes and is not windowed: both ends are local processes.
 */
class IpcLink extends BaseLink {
	         #channel            ;
	         #onMessage = (message         )       => {
		const frame = (message                               )?.chord2;
		if (frame === undefined) return;
		try {
			this.deliver(unpackObject(frame)         );
		} catch {
			this.close("protocol error");
		}
	};
	         #onDisconnect = ()       => this.ended("ipc disconnected");

	constructor(channel            ) {
		super();
		this.#channel = channel;
		channel.on("message", this.#onMessage);
		channel.on("disconnect", this.#onDisconnect);
	}

	          transmit(frame       )            {
		if (this.#channel.connected) this.#channel.send({ chord2: packObject(frame, true) });
		return undefined;
	}

	          shutdown()       {
		this.#channel.off("message", this.#onMessage);
		this.#channel.off("disconnect", this.#onDisconnect);
	}
}

const ipcLink = (channel            )          => new IpcLink(channel);

// ─── WebSocket ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** The WHATWG WebSocket surface: the browser `WebSocket`, Node's global `WebSocket`, and `ws` sockets. */
                                
	                            
	                                      
	                                            
	                                                                                               
	                                                                               
 

const OPEN = 1;

                                       
	                                                                                                      
	                            
	                                                                                                          
	                                
 

class WebSocketLink extends BaseLink {
	         #socket               ;
	         #compress         ;
	#outbox                          = [];

	constructor(socket               , options                       = {}) {
		super();
		this.#socket = socket;
		this.#compress = options.compress ?? false;
		const acceptPacked = options.acceptPacked ?? true;
		// Binary messages as ArrayBuffer (browsers and Node's WebSocket default to Blob).
		if ("binaryType" in socket) (socket                          ).binaryType = "arraybuffer";
		socket.addEventListener("open", () => {
			for (const data of this.#outbox.splice(0)) socket.send(data);
		});
		socket.addEventListener("message", (event) => {
			try {
				const data = event.data;
				if (typeof data === "string") {
					this.deliver(parseFrame(data));
					return;
				}
				if (!acceptPacked) throw new TypeError("packed frames are not accepted");
				const bytes =
					data instanceof ArrayBuffer
						? new Uint8Array(data)
						: ArrayBuffer.isView(data)
							? new Uint8Array(data.buffer, data.byteOffset, data.byteLength)
							: undefined;
				if (bytes === undefined) throw new TypeError("unknown message type");
				const { envelope, field, bytes: payload } = splitMessage(bytes);
				if (zlib !== undefined) this.deliver({ ...envelope, [field]: new Packed(payload) }         );
				else this.deliverLater(inflate(payload).then((value) => ({ ...envelope, [field]: value })         ));
			} catch {
				this.close("protocol error");
			}
		});
		socket.addEventListener("close", () => this.ended("socket closed"));
	}

	          transmit(frame       )         {
		const data = packMessage(frame, this.#compress);
		if (this.#socket.readyState === OPEN) this.#socket.send(data);
		else this.#outbox.push(data);
		return typeof data === "string" ? data.length : data.byteLength;
	}

	          shutdown()       {
		this.#outbox = [];
		if (this.#socket.readyState <= OPEN) this.#socket.close(1000);
	}
}

const webSocketLink = (socket               , options                       )                =>
	new WebSocketLink(socket, options);

// ══ connection.ts ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const DEFAULT_WINDOW = 256 * 1024;
/** A waiting subscription keeps at most this many windows of ops; beyond, its current value replaces them. */
const PENDING_WINDOWS = 4;

                                                 

/**
 * What waits for one subscription: a snapshot, reset or error (`base`), then encoded ops to apply after it, in order.
 * Ops stay as they arrived (the receiving decoder validates them).
 */
                                                                                                

                                                    
	                                                                                                              
	                              
	   
	                                                                                                                  
	                                                                                 
	   
	                                  
	                                                                                     
	                       
	                                                                                                        
	                                                                   
	   
	                                                                                                                  
	                                                                                 
	   
	                           
 

                       
	                                 
	                                                                                   
	                                  
 

/** A hold the peer made on this host: served locally or relayed to another link. */
             
	                                                                                                                     
	                                                        

                                                                                                         

const unavailable = (message        )             => new ChordError("unavailable", message);

// ─── relay ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * A hold relayed from an origin link to a downstream link. It owns everything done through it: subscriptions and
 * calls queued while the route resolves, and the downstream twins once forwarded. `close` undoes all of it exactly
 * once (cancels calls downstream, drops subscriptions, releases the downstream hold, rejects waiters).
 */
class RelayHold {
	         origin            ;
	         frame                               ;
	         principal           ;
	         client         ;
	/** The origin's authorization generation when the hold arrived; a revocation bumps it. */
	         generation        ;
	down                        ;
	downId = 0;
	closed = false;
	         calls = new Map                   ();
	         subs = new Map                                                                 ();
	         #waiters = new Set         ();

	constructor(origin            , frame                               , principal           , client         ) {
		this.origin = origin;
		this.frame = frame;
		this.principal = principal;
		this.client = client;
		this.generation = origin.generation;
	}

	get originId()         {
		return this.frame.id;
	}

	/** Still authorized as it was when it arrived. */
	get current()          {
		return !this.closed && this.origin.open && this.origin.generation === this.generation;
	}

	/** The downstream connection once the route resolved; rejects if the relay closes first. */
	ready()                      {
		if (this.closed) return Promise.reject(new ChordError("gone", "relayed hold closed"));
		if (this.down !== undefined) return Promise.resolve(this.down);
		return new Promise            ((resolve, reject) => {
			this.#waiters.add({ resolve: resolve                            , reject });
		});
	}

	/** Forwards the hold (and what queued meanwhile) to `down`. */
	open(down            )       {
		if (this.closed) return;
		this.down = down;
		this.downId = down.nextId();
		down.relayedHolds.set(this.downId, this);
		down.send({
			...this.frame,
			id: this.downId,
			principal: this.principal,
			...(this.client ? { client: true } : {}),
		});
		for (const [originSubId, entry] of this.subs)
			if (entry.downId === undefined) this.#forwardSub(originSubId, entry);
		for (const waiter of this.#waiters) waiter.resolve(down);
		this.#waiters.clear();
	}

	subscribe(originSubId        , member        )       {
		const entry = { member, downId: undefined                       };
		this.subs.set(originSubId, entry);
		this.origin.relaySubs.set(originSubId, this);
		if (this.down !== undefined) this.#forwardSub(originSubId, entry);
	}

	#forwardSub(originSubId        , entry                                                         )       {
		const down = this.down ;
		entry.downId = down.nextId();
		down.relayedSubs.set(entry.downId, { relay: this, originSubId });
		down.send({ t: "sub", id: entry.downId, hold: this.downId, member: entry.member });
	}

	/**
	 * The origin could not keep up and dropped what waited for this subscription: subscribe downstream anew, so the
	 * provider's snapshot (a reset for the origin's consumer) replaces the ops that were lost.
	 */
	resync(originSubId        )       {
		const entry = this.subs.get(originSubId);
		this.origin.dropState(originSubId);
		if (entry?.downId === undefined || this.down === undefined) return;
		this.down.relayedSubs.delete(entry.downId);
		this.down.send({ t: "unsub", id: entry.downId });
		this.#forwardSub(originSubId, entry);
	}

	unsubscribe(originSubId        , forward = true)       {
		const entry = this.subs.get(originSubId);
		if (entry === undefined) return;
		this.subs.delete(originSubId);
		this.origin.relaySubs.delete(originSubId);
		this.origin.dropState(originSubId);
		if (entry.downId !== undefined && this.down !== undefined) {
			this.down.relayedSubs.delete(entry.downId);
			if (forward) this.down.send({ t: "unsub", id: entry.downId });
		}
	}

	/**
	 * Closes the relay. `releaseDown`: the downstream hold is still there (origin released, disconnected, or was
	 * revoked): cancel its calls and release it. `notify`: tell the origin the hold is gone.
	 */
	close(error         , options                                                             )       {
		if (this.closed) return;
		this.closed = true;
		for (const call of [...this.calls.values()]) call.cancel(error, options.releaseDown);
		for (const originSubId of [...this.subs.keys()]) this.unsubscribe(originSubId, options.releaseDown);
		if (this.origin.served.get(this.originId)?.kind === "relay") this.origin.served.delete(this.originId);
		if (this.down !== undefined) {
			this.down.relayedHolds.delete(this.downId);
			if (options.releaseDown) this.down.send({ t: "release", id: this.downId });
		}
		for (const waiter of this.#waiters) waiter.reject(error);
		this.#waiters.clear();
		if (options.notify) this.origin.send({ t: "gone", id: this.originId, error: toWireError(error) });
	}
}

/** One relayed call: settles once, by result, cancellation or the relay closing; removes both mappings. */
class RelayCall {
	         relay           ;
	         originId        ;
	         downId        ;
	         #pending         ;
	#settled = false;

	constructor(relay           , originId        , downId        , pending         ) {
		this.relay = relay;
		this.originId = originId;
		this.downId = downId;
		this.#pending = pending;
		relay.calls.set(originId, this);
		relay.down?.relayedCalls.set(downId, this);
	}

	finish(error         , value          )       {
		if (this.#settled) return;
		this.#settled = true;
		this.relay.calls.delete(this.originId);
		this.relay.down?.relayedCalls.delete(this.downId);
		if (error === undefined) this.#pending.resolve(value);
		else this.#pending.reject(error);
	}

	/** Cancels downstream (when the downstream hold still exists) and settles with `error`. */
	cancel(error         , forward         )       {
		if (this.#settled) return;
		if (forward) this.relay.down?.send({ t: "cancel", id: this.downId });
		this.finish(error);
	}
}

// ─── connection ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * One end of a link on a host: consumer of remote handles, provider for the peer's holds, and relay.
 *
 * Flow control
 * ------------
 *
 * Problem: state is pushed as fast as it changes, but a peer may read slowly (a phone behind a relay), and a relay may
 * hide that: Cloudflare forwards to browsers without backpressure. Everything sent then queues in front of the next
 * call result. Example: a browser opens three long transcripts (500 KiB of snapshots each) over a 5 Mbit/s path; a
 * result sent next waits 2.4 s, and the snapshot of a transcript the user already left is still sent in full.
 *
 * Solution: the peer acknowledges what it processed (`ack`, a cumulative frame count), and the sender counts the sizes
 * of the frames not yet acknowledged. While they exceed the window, *state* frames wait here, one slot per
 * subscription: ops are appended to the slot (encoded batches of one stream may be concatenated, see `encoder`), and
 * a snapshot, reset or error replaces it. Unsubscribing drops the slot. A slot whose ops outgrow `PENDING_WINDOWS`
 * windows is replaced by the current value (a reset), or, on a relay, by a fresh downstream subscription (`resync`),
 * so memory stays bounded however long the peer stalls. Acknowledgements send the slots in the order they were
 * created, as long as the window allows.
 *
 * Everything else (results, shapes, gone, reload control, pings) is sent at once: it is bounded by the requests the
 * peer makes, and it must not wait behind state. Hence the one ordering exception of a link: while state waits, a
 * result can arrive before state changes made before it. Frames of one subscription stay in order.
 *
 * The window bounds what is unacknowledged when a frame is sent, so one frame (a large snapshot) may exceed it. Both
 * hellos must announce `acks`; with an older peer nothing is acknowledged and nothing waits. Links that do not report
 * sizes (IPC) never fill the window.
 */
class Connection                          {
	         host          ;
	         link      ;
	/** `upstream`: the link to this host's parent. `downstream`: a link this host accepted. */
	         role                           ;
	         peer      ;
	principal           ;
	         restamp                                                               ;
	/** Bumped when the stamped principal is revoked or re-stamped: relays from before are refused. */
	generation = 0;
	/** The epoch a downstream host peer runs (from its hello, then from commits). */
	peerEpoch                    ;
	/** A downstream host peer is usable once it runs this host's current epoch (see `HostImpl.peerHello`). */
	synced         ;
	/** Epochs this child announced while behind this host (fenced): this host never ran them. */
	         fencedEpochs = new Set        ();
	/** Roots of fenced epochs, by the epoch of this host they were admitted in. */
	         #admitted = new Map                ();
	#parentName                    ;
	         #syncWaiters = new Set         ();
	#nextId = 1;
	#lastSeen = Date.now();
	#pinger                                            ;
	         #maxCalls        ;
	// flow control (see the class comment)
	         #window        ;
	/** Whether the peer acknowledges frames; until its hello is here, frames are counted in case it does. */
	#peerAcks                     ;
	#sent = 0;
	#acked = 0;
	/** Sizes of the frames sent after the last acknowledged one. */
	#unacked           = [];
	#unackedBytes = 0;
	         #pending = new Map                      ();
	#received = 0;
	#ackTimer                                           ;
	// consumer side: what this host asked of the peer
	         remotes = new Map                      ();
	         calls = new Map                 ();
	         subs = new Map                                    ();
	// provider side: what the peer asked of this host
	         served = new Map                ();
	         incoming = new Map                         ();
	         feeds = new Map                    ();
	/** Origin side of relays: the peer's sub ids that are relayed. */
	         relaySubs = new Map                   ();
	// downstream side of relays: ids this host used on this link
	         relayedHolds = new Map                   ();
	         relayedCalls = new Map                   ();
	         relayedSubs = new Map                                                                     ();
	/** Reload votes awaited from this (child) peer. */
	         votes = new Map                 ();

	constructor(
		host          ,
		link      ,
		config   
			                                         
			                              
			                    
			                                                                   
			                           
		               ,
	) {
		this.host = host;
		this.link = link;
		this.role = config.role;
		this.peer = config.peer;
		this.principal = config.principal;
		this.restamp = config.restamp;
		this.synced = !(this.role === "downstream" && this.peer.kind === "host");
		this.#window = config.window ?? DEFAULT_WINDOW;
		this.#maxCalls = config.maxCalls ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
		link.onClose((reason) => this.#closed(reason));
		link.receive((frame) => this.#receive(frame));
		this.send({ t: "hello", name: host.name, kind: host.kind, epoch: host.current.id, acks: true });
		const interval = config.pingInterval ?? 15_000;
		const timeout = config.pingTimeout ?? 45_000;
		// A link that was already closed closed this connection above; it needs no keepalive.
		if (link.closed) return;
		let n = 0;
		this.#pinger = setInterval(() => {
			if (Date.now() - this.#lastSeen > timeout) link.close("ping timeout");
			else this.send({ t: "ping", n: ++n });
		}, interval);
		this.#pinger.unref?.();
	}

	get open()          {
		return !this.link.closed;
	}

	/** The peer's name: configured for downstream hosts, announced by the parent upstream. */
	get name()                     {
		return this.role === "upstream" ? this.#parentName : this.peer.name;
	}

	send(frame       )       {
		const size = this.link.send(frame) ?? 0;
		if (this.#peerAcks === false) return;
		this.#sent++;
		this.#unacked.push(size);
		this.#unackedBytes += size;
	}

	/** Sends a state frame, or keeps it waiting (see the class comment). False: the slot is full, send a reset. */
	sendState(frame            )          {
		let slot = this.#pending.get(frame.id);
		if (slot === undefined && !this.#full) {
			this.send(frame);
			return true;
		}
		if (slot === undefined) {
			slot = { base: undefined, ops: [], size: 0 };
			this.#pending.set(frame.id, slot);
		}
		if (frame.ops === undefined) {
			slot.base = frame;
			slot.ops = [];
			slot.size = 0;
			return true;
		}
		const size = JSON.stringify(frame.ops).length;
		if (slot.size + size > this.#window * PENDING_WINDOWS) return false;
		for (const op of frame.ops) slot.ops.push(op);
		slot.size += size;
		return true;
	}

	/** Forgets what waits for a subscription that ended. */
	dropState(id        )       {
		this.#pending.delete(id);
	}

	get #full()          {
		return this.#peerAcks === true && this.#unackedBytes >= this.#window;
	}

	#onAck(n        )       {
		if (this.#peerAcks !== true || n < this.#acked || n > this.#sent) {
			this.#violation(`ack ${n} outside ${this.#acked}..${this.#sent}`);
			return;
		}
		for (const size of this.#unacked.splice(0, n - this.#acked)) this.#unackedBytes -= size;
		this.#acked = n;
		for (const [id, slot] of this.#pending) {
			if (this.#full) return;
			this.#pending.delete(id);
			if (slot.base !== undefined) this.send(slot.base);
			if (slot.ops.length > 0) this.send({ t: "state", id, ops: slot.ops });
		}
	}

	/** Acknowledges once the frames that arrived together are processed: one ack per burst, not per frame. */
	#scheduleAck()       {
		if (this.#peerAcks !== true || this.#ackTimer !== undefined || !this.open) return;
		this.#ackTimer = setTimeout(() => {
			this.#ackTimer = undefined;
			this.link.send({ t: "ack", n: this.#received });
		}, 0);
		this.#ackTimer.unref?.();
	}

	nextId()         {
		return this.#nextId++;
	}

	/** Resolves once the peer is usable (a host peer runs this host's epoch); rejects if the link closes first. */
	whenSynced()                {
		if (!this.open) return Promise.reject(unavailable(`link to ${this.name ?? "peer"} closed`));
		if (this.synced) return Promise.resolve();
		return new Promise      ((resolve, reject) => this.#syncWaiters.add({ resolve: resolve              , reject }));
	}

	markSynced(epoch        )       {
		this.peerEpoch = epoch;
		this.synced = true;
		for (const waiter of this.#syncWaiters) waiter.resolve(undefined);
		this.#syncWaiters.clear();
	}

	/** Revokes the stamped principal: relays made under it close at once (also those still resolving a route). */
	revoke(next                       )       {
		this.generation++;
		const error = new ChordError("gone", "principal invalidated");
		for (const served of [...this.served.values()]) {
			if (served.kind === "relay") served.relay.close(error, { releaseDown: true, notify: true });
		}
		if (next === undefined) this.link.close("principal invalidated");
		else this.principal = next;
	}

	/** A protocol violation: the peer cannot be trusted with anything on this link any more. */
	#violation(reason        )       {
		this.host.report(new ChordError("invalid", `link to ${this.name ?? "peer"}: ${reason}`, { reason: "protocol" }));
		this.link.close(`protocol error: ${reason}`);
	}

	// ─── consumer side (RemoteChannel) ───

	hold(handle              )         {
		const id = this.nextId();
		this.remotes.set(id, handle);
		handle.bound(this, id);
		const principal = handle.principal;
		this.send({
			t: "hold",
			id,
			at: handle.at,
			token: handle.contract.description,
			input: handle.input,
			...(principal === undefined ? {} : { principal }),
			...(handle.owner === "client" ? { client: true } : {}),
		});
		return id;
	}

	release(id        )       {
		this.remotes.delete(id);
		this.send({ t: "release", id });
	}

	call(hold        , member        , args         , root                     , signal             )                   {
		const id = this.nextId();
		return new Promise         ((resolve, reject) => {
			if (signal.aborted) {
				reject(new ChordError("aborted", "call aborted"));
				return;
			}
			const onAbort = ()       => {
				this.calls.delete(id);
				this.send({ t: "cancel", id });
				reject(new ChordError("aborted", "call aborted", { cause: signal.reason }));
			};
			signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
			const done = ()       => signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
			this.calls.set(id, {
				resolve: (value) => {
					done();
					resolve(value);
				},
				reject: (error) => {
					done();
					reject(error);
				},
			});
			// Clients never carry roots or epochs: the server starts roots for them.
			const carried = root !== undefined && this.host.kind === "host";
			if (carried) this.host.rootSent(root.rootId, this);
			this.send({ t: "call", id, hold, member, args, ...(carried ? { root } : {}) });
		});
	}

	sub(hold        , member        , sink                            )         {
		const id = this.nextId();
		this.subs.set(id, sink);
		this.send({ t: "sub", id, hold, member });
		return id;
	}

	unsub(id        )       {
		if (this.subs.delete(id)) this.send({ t: "unsub", id });
	}

	// ─── receiving ───

	#receive(frame         )       {
		this.#lastSeen = Date.now();
		if (!isFrame(frame)) {
			this.#violation("malformed frame");
			return;
		}
		if (frame.t === "ack") {
			this.#onAck(frame.n);
			return;
		}
		this.#received++;
		try {
			this.#dispatch(frame);
		} catch (error) {
			this.host.report(error);
		}
		this.#scheduleAck();
	}

	#dispatch(frame       )       {
		switch (frame.t) {
			case "hello":
				this.#onHello(frame);
				return;
			case "ping":
				this.send({ t: "pong", n: frame.n });
				return;
			case "pong":
			case "bye":
			case "ack":
				return;
			case "hold":
				this.#onHold(frame);
				return;
			case "release":
				this.#dropServed(frame.id, true);
				return;
			case "call":
				this.#onCall(frame);
				return;
			case "cancel":
				this.incoming.get(frame.id)?.abort(new ChordError("aborted", "cancelled by caller"));
				return;
			case "sub":
				this.#onSub(frame);
				return;
			case "unsub":
				this.#onUnsub(frame.id);
				return;
			case "shape":
			case "gone":
				this.#onHoldReply(frame);
				return;
			case "result":
				this.#onResult(frame);
				return;
			case "state":
				this.#onState(frame);
				return;
			case "root_end":
				// Honored only from the link the root came over (see HostImpl.foreignRootEnded).
				this.#admitted.delete(frame.rootId);
				this.host.foreignRootEnded(frame.rootId, this);
				return;
			case "invalidate":
			case "prepare":
			case "commit":
			case "abort":
			case "retire":
				// Control frames only from the parent.
				if (this.role !== "upstream") {
					this.#violation(`${frame.t} from a child`);
					return;
				}
				if (frame.t === "invalidate") {
					this.host.invalidatePrincipal(frame.pattern).catch((error         ) => this.host.report(error));
				} else if (frame.t === "retire") this.host.allowRetire(frame.epoch);
				else this.host.onReloadFrame(this, frame);
				return;
			case "vote": {
				if (this.role !== "downstream" || this.peer.kind !== "host") {
					this.#violation("vote from a non-participant");
					return;
				}
				const vote = this.votes.get(frame.reload);
				this.votes.delete(frame.reload);
				if (frame.error === undefined) vote?.resolve(frame.report);
				else vote?.reject(fromWireError(frame.error));
				return;
			}
		}
	}

	/**
	 * The peer's hello must agree with what this side decided about it. A downstream host announces its epoch: the
	 * host either finds it in step or fences and reconciles it (never renumbers).
	 */
	#onHello(frame                                )       {
		if (this.#peerAcks === undefined) {
			this.#peerAcks = frame.acks === true;
			if (!this.#peerAcks) {
				this.#unacked = [];
				this.#unackedBytes = 0;
			}
		}
		if (this.role === "upstream") {
			if (frame.kind !== "host") {
				this.#violation("parent is not a host");
				return;
			}
			this.#parentName ??= frame.name;
			return;
		}
		if (frame.kind !== this.peer.kind || (this.peer.name !== undefined && frame.name !== this.peer.name)) {
			this.#violation(
				`hello ${frame.kind} ${frame.name} does not match the accepted ${this.peer.kind} ${this.peer.name ?? ""}`,
			);
			return;
		}
		if (this.peer.kind === "host") this.host.peerHello(this, frame.epoch);
	}

	/**
	 * Principal stamping: a downstream link acts as the principal it was accepted with, whatever the frame says. Only
	 * the parent may forward a principal (relayed requests); its own requests act as `{ host: parentName }`.
	 */
	#principalOf(frame                                    )            {
		if (this.role === "downstream") return this.principal;
		return frame.principal ?? { host: this.#parentName ?? "parent" };
	}

	#isClient(frame                               )          {
		return this.role === "downstream" ? this.peer.kind === "client" : frame.client === true;
	}

	#onHold(frame                               )       {
		if (this.served.has(frame.id)) {
			this.#violation(`duplicate hold id ${frame.id}`);
			return;
		}
		const principal = this.#principalOf(frame);
		const client = this.#isClient(frame);
		if (frame.at !== this.host.name) {
			if (this.role === "upstream") {
				this.send({ t: "gone", id: frame.id, error: toWireError(unavailable(`${this.host.name} does not relay`)) });
				return;
			}
			const relay = new RelayHold(this, frame, principal, client);
			this.served.set(frame.id, { kind: "relay", relay });
			this.host.relay(relay).catch((error         ) => relay.close(error, { releaseDown: true, notify: true }));
			return;
		}
		let handle            ;
		try {
			const token = contractFromDescription(frame.token);
			if (token.scope !== "remote") throw invalid("token", `${token.name} is local; only remote types cross links`);
			const acquired = this.host.acquire(token, frame.input, { principal }, client ? "client" : "connection");
			if (!(acquired instanceof HandleImpl)) throw unavailable("not a local handle");
			handle = acquired;
		} catch (error) {
			this.send({ t: "gone", id: frame.id, error: toWireError(client ? clientError(error) : error) });
			return;
		}
		const served         = { kind: "local", handle, principal, feeds: new Set() };
		this.served.set(frame.id, served);
		const shape = ()       => {
			const current = handle.shape;
			if (current === undefined || this.served.get(frame.id) !== served) return;
			this.send({
				t: "shape",
				id: frame.id,
				kind: current.kind,
				members: current.members,
				contract: current.contract,
				epoch: current.epoch,
			});
		};
		handle.onShape(shape);
		handle.goneListeners.add((error) => {
			if (this.served.get(frame.id) !== served) return;
			this.#dropServed(frame.id, false);
			this.send({ t: "gone", id: frame.id, error: toWireError(error) });
		});
		if (handle.status === "live") shape();
	}

	/** Forgets a served hold: unsubscribes its feeds and releases it (locally or downstream). */
	#dropServed(id        , release         )       {
		const served = this.served.get(id);
		if (served === undefined) return;
		this.served.delete(id);
		if (served.kind === "relay") {
			served.relay.close(new ChordError("released", "relayed hold released"), {
				releaseDown: release,
				notify: false,
			});
			return;
		}
		for (const feed of served.feeds) this.#onUnsub(feed);
		if (release) served.handle.release();
	}

	#onCall(frame                               )       {
		if (this.incoming.has(frame.id)) {
			this.#violation(`duplicate call id ${frame.id}`);
			return;
		}
		if (this.incoming.size >= this.#maxCalls) {
			const error = new ChordError(
				"unavailable",
				`Too many calls in progress on this connection (${this.#maxCalls}); wait for earlier ones to finish`,
				{ reason: "too_many_calls" },
			);
			this.send({ t: "result", id: frame.id, error: toWireError(error) });
			return;
		}
		const served = this.served.get(frame.hold);
		if (served === undefined) {
			this.send({ t: "result", id: frame.id, error: toWireError(new ChordError("gone", "unknown hold")) });
			return;
		}
		const controller = new AbortController();
		this.incoming.set(frame.id, controller);
		// Roots are honored from hosts only; a request without one (or from a client) starts one here.
		const root = this.peer.kind === "host" ? this.#admitRoot(frame.root) : undefined;
		const principal = served.kind === "local" ? served.principal : served.relay.principal;
		const options = { principal, signal: controller.signal };
		const run =
			served.kind === "local"
				? (ctx     ) => served.handle.record.call(served.handle, frame.member, unpack(frame.args), ctx           )
				: (ctx     ) =>
						relayCall(
							served.relay,
							frame,
							{ rootId: ctx.rootId, epoch: ctx.epoch },
							controller.signal,
							this.host,
						);
		const result =
			root === undefined
				? this.host.root(run, options)
				: this.host.joinRoot(root.rootId, root.epoch, run, { ...options, from: this });
		result
			.then(
				(value) => this.send({ t: "result", id: frame.id, value }),
				(error         ) => this.send({ t: "result", id: frame.id, error: toWireError(error) }),
			)
			.finally(() => this.incoming.delete(frame.id));
	}

	/**
	 * A child that connected behind this host runs roots of an epoch this host never had (it was fenced and is being,
	 * or was, reconciled). Such a root is admitted in this host's current epoch at its first call here, and keeps that
	 * epoch for its later calls (`superseded` once this host retired it). Other unknown epochs stay `superseded`.
	 */
	#admitRoot(root                     )                      {
		if (root === undefined || this.role !== "downstream" || !this.fencedEpochs.has(root.epoch)) return root;
		if (this.host.hasEpoch(root.epoch)) return root;
		let epoch = this.#admitted.get(root.rootId);
		if (epoch === undefined) {
			epoch = this.host.current.id;
			this.#admitted.set(root.rootId, epoch);
		}
		return { rootId: root.rootId, epoch };
	}

	#onSub(frame                              )       {
		if (this.feeds.has(frame.id) || this.relaySubs.has(frame.id)) {
			this.#violation(`duplicate sub id ${frame.id}`);
			return;
		}
		const served = this.served.get(frame.hold);
		if (served === undefined) {
			this.send({ t: "state", id: frame.id, error: toWireError(new ChordError("gone", "unknown hold")) });
			return;
		}
		if (served.kind === "relay") {
			served.relay.subscribe(frame.id, frame.member);
			return;
		}
		const readable = served.handle.member(frame.member)                                 ;
		if (readable === undefined || typeof readable.events !== "function") {
			this.send({
				t: "state",
				id: frame.id,
				error: toWireError(new ChordError("not_in_variant", `${frame.member} is not state`)),
			});
			return;
		}
		let stream          = encoder();
		const send = (event                     )       => {
			if (event.type === "ops") {
				if (this.sendState({ t: "state", id: frame.id, ops: stream.encode(event.ops) })) return;
				// Too many ops wait for a slow peer: the current value replaces them.
				event = { type: "reset", value: event.value };
			}
			// A loading provider handle has nothing yet: the hydrated value follows as a reset.
			if (event.value === undefined) return;
			// Snapshots are validated against the effective schema once per (re)subscription; ops only in dev mode.
			try {
				validateSnapshot(served.handle, frame.member, event.value);
			} catch (error) {
				this.sendState({ t: "state", id: frame.id, error: toWireError(error) });
				return;
			}
			stream = encoder();
			this.sendState(
				event.type === "snap"
					? { t: "state", id: frame.id, snap: event.value }
					: { t: "state", id: frame.id, reset: event.value },
			);
		};
		const unsubscribe = readable.events(send);
		this.feeds.set(frame.id, unsubscribe);
		served.feeds.add(frame.id);
	}

	#onUnsub(id        )       {
		this.dropState(id);
		const unsubscribe = this.feeds.get(id);
		if (unsubscribe !== undefined) {
			this.feeds.delete(id);
			unsubscribe();
		}
		this.relaySubs.get(id)?.unsubscribe(id);
	}

	#onHoldReply(frame                                         )       {
		const relay = this.relayedHolds.get(frame.id);
		if (relay !== undefined) {
			if (frame.t === "gone") relay.close(fromWireError(frame.error), { releaseDown: false, notify: true });
			else if (!relay.closed) relay.origin.send({ ...frame, id: relay.originId });
			return;
		}
		const handle = this.remotes.get(frame.id);
		if (handle === undefined) return;
		if (frame.t === "gone") {
			this.remotes.delete(frame.id);
			handle.gone(fromWireError(frame.error));
			return;
		}
		handle.shaped({ kind: frame.kind, members: frame.members, contract: frame.contract, epoch: frame.epoch });
	}

	#onResult(frame                                 )       {
		const relayed = this.relayedCalls.get(frame.id);
		if (relayed !== undefined) {
			relayed.finish(frame.error === undefined ? undefined : fromWireError(frame.error), frame.value);
			return;
		}
		const pending = this.calls.get(frame.id);
		this.calls.delete(frame.id);
		if (frame.error !== undefined) {
			pending?.reject(fromWireError(frame.error));
			return;
		}
		let value         ;
		try {
			value = unpack(frame.value);
		} catch (error) {
			pending?.reject(error);
			return;
		}
		pending?.resolve(value);
	}

	#onState(frame                                )       {
		const relayed = this.relayedSubs.get(frame.id);
		if (relayed !== undefined) {
			if (!relayed.relay.origin.sendState({ ...frame, id: relayed.originSubId })) {
				relayed.relay.resync(relayed.originSubId);
			}
			return;
		}
		const sink = this.subs.get(frame.id);
		if (sink === undefined) return;
		if (!(frame.snap instanceof Packed) && !(frame.reset instanceof Packed)) {
			sink(frame);
			return;
		}
		let unpacked           ;
		try {
			unpacked =
				frame.snap !== undefined
					? { ...frame, snap: unpack(frame.snap) }
					: { ...frame, reset: unpack(frame.reset) };
		} catch (error) {
			unpacked = { error: toWireError(error) };
		}
		sink(unpacked);
	}

	// ─── lifetime ───

	#closed(reason        )       {
		if (this.#pinger !== undefined) clearInterval(this.#pinger);
		if (this.#ackTimer !== undefined) clearTimeout(this.#ackTimer);
		this.#pending.clear();
		const error = unavailable(`link to ${this.name ?? "peer"} closed: ${reason}`);
