		// As origin: everything the peer held here, relayed or not, is released (downstream calls cancelled).
		for (const id of [...this.served.keys()]) this.#dropServed(id, true);
		for (const controller of this.incoming.values()) controller.abort(error);
		this.incoming.clear();
		for (const pending of this.calls.values()) pending.reject(error);
		this.calls.clear();
		this.subs.clear();
		for (const vote of this.votes.values()) vote.reject(error);
		this.votes.clear();
		for (const waiter of this.#syncWaiters) waiter.reject(error);
		this.#syncWaiters.clear();
		// As downstream of relays: their origins lose the hold.
		for (const relay of [...this.relayedHolds.values()]) relay.close(error, { releaseDown: false, notify: true });
		this.relayedHolds.clear();
		this.relayedCalls.clear();
		this.relayedSubs.clear();
		for (const handle of this.remotes.values()) {
			// An upstream link may be replaced (reconnect); a downstream host that went away is gone.
			if (this.role === "upstream") handle.detached();
			else handle.gone(error);
		}
		this.remotes.clear();
		this.host.connectionClosed(this);
	}
}

/**
 * A call through a relay: waits for the route, then forwards with the root it runs in. It settles exactly once; a
 * cancel (or the origin going away) is forwarded downstream and removes both mappings.
 */
async function relayCall(
	relay           ,
	frame                               ,
	root         ,
	signal             ,
	host          ,
)                   {
	const abortError = ()             => new ChordError("aborted", "call cancelled", { cause: signal.reason });
	if (signal.aborted) throw abortError();
	let onAbort = ()       => {};
	const aborted = new Promise       ((_, reject) => {
		onAbort = () => reject(abortError());
		signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
	});
	aborted.catch(() => {});
	try {
		const down = await Promise.race([relay.ready(), aborted]);
		if (relay.closed) throw new ChordError("gone", "relayed hold closed");
		return await new Promise         ((resolve, reject) => {
			const call = new RelayCall(relay, frame.id, down.nextId(), { resolve, reject });
			signal.addEventListener("abort", () => call.cancel(abortError(), true), { once: true });
			host.rootSent(root.rootId, down);
			down.send({ t: "call", id: call.downId, hold: relay.downId, member: frame.member, args: frame.args, root });
		});
	} finally {
		signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
	}
}

/**
 * Validates a snapshot against the schema the consumer is promised: the provider's own schema for a direct binding,
 * the consumer's contract for one through an `accepts` adapter (the adapter's view is what the consumer gets).
 */
function validateSnapshot(handle            , member        , value         )       {
	if (value === undefined) return;
	const installed = handle.record.currentInstalled();
	if (installed === undefined) return;
	const view = handle.currentView();
	const kind = view === undefined ? kindOf(installed.behavior) : kindOf(view);
	const description =
		view !== undefined && view !== installed.behavior
			? handle.contract.description.variants[kind ?? LOCAL_VARIANT]?.[member]
			: installed.service.contract.description.variants[kindOf(installed.behavior) ?? LOCAL_VARIANT]?.[member];
	if (description?.kind === "state") assertValid("state", `${handle.address}.${member}`, description.schema, value);
}

/** Clients (browsers) holding an outdated contract are told to reload their code. */
function clientError(error         )          {
	if (error instanceof ChordError && error.code === "contract_mismatch") {
		return new ChordError("upgrade_required", error.message, { cause: error });
	}
	return error;
}

// ══ remote.ts ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const remoteNoop = ()       => {};

                            
	               
	                
	                         
	                  
 

/** What a remote handle needs from the link it is bound to (implemented by Connection). */
                                
	                                   
	                          
	                                                                                                                    
	                                                                            
	                        
 

/** What a remote handle needs from its host. */
                             
	                           
	                             
	                                                                                          
	                                         
 

/**
 * A state member of a remote handle: hydrated while subscribed (snapshot, then decoded ops); `undefined` before.
 * Re-subscribing after a reconnect delivers the new snapshot as a reset.
 */
class RemoteReadable                              {
	         #handle              ;
	         #name        ;
	         #values = new Set                          ();
	         #events = new Set                                      ();
	#sub                                                                      ;
	#decoder          = decoder();
	#value         ;

	constructor(handle              , name        ) {
		this.#handle = handle;
		this.#name = name;
	}

	get value()          {
		return this.#value;
	}

	subscribe(listener                          )             {
		this.#values.add(listener);
		this.#safe(() => listener(this.#value));
		this.bind();
		return () => this.#remove(this.#values, listener);
	}

	events(listener                                      )             {
		this.#events.add(listener);
		this.#safe(() => listener({ type: "snap", value: this.#value }));
		this.bind();
		return () => this.#remove(this.#events, listener);
	}

	/** Subscribes upstream while someone listens and the handle is bound; drops a subscription on an old link. */
	bind()       {
		const listening = this.#values.size + this.#events.size > 0;
		// Nobody listens: the value would go stale, so forget it. The next subscriber sees `undefined`, then the snapshot.
		if (!listening) this.#value = undefined;
		const binding = listening ? this.#handle.binding : undefined;
		if (binding !== undefined && this.#sub?.channel === binding.channel) return;
		if (this.#sub !== undefined) {
			if (this.#sub.channel !== binding?.channel || binding === undefined) this.#sub.channel.unsub(this.#sub.id);
			this.#sub = undefined;
		}
		if (binding === undefined) return;
		const channel = binding.channel;
		this.#decoder = decoder();
		const id = channel.sub(binding.id, this.#name, (frame) => this.#receive(frame));
		this.#sub = { channel, id };
	}

	/** The link is gone: keep the last value, resubscribe on the next binding. */
	detach()       {
		this.#sub = undefined;
	}

	stop()       {
		this.#values.clear();
		this.#events.clear();
		this.bind();
	}

	#receive(frame           )       {
		if (frame.error !== undefined) {
			this.#handle.host.report(fromWireError(frame.error));
			return;
		}
		let event                     ;
		if (frame.ops === undefined && !("snap" in frame) && !("reset" in frame)) return;
		if (frame.ops !== undefined) {
			const ops = this.#decoder.decode(frame.ops);
			this.#value = applyImmutable(this.#value, ops);
			event = { type: "ops", ops, value: this.#value };
		} else {
			// Listeners already saw a snapshot (`undefined` or an older value), so every upstream snapshot is a reset.
			this.#decoder = decoder();
			this.#value = "snap" in frame ? frame.snap : frame.reset;
			event = { type: "reset", value: this.#value };
		}
		for (const listener of [...this.#events]) this.#safe(() => listener(event));
		for (const listener of [...this.#values]) this.#safe(() => listener(this.#value));
	}

	#remove   (set        , listener   )       {
		set.delete(listener);
		this.bind();
	}

	#safe(fn            )       {
		try {
			fn();
		} catch (error) {
			this.#handle.host.report(error);
		}
	}
}

/** A hold on an object on another host, bound to a link. Late-bound like a local handle. */
class RemoteHandle                 {
	         record = undefined;
	         host            ;
	         contract             ;
	         at        ;
	         input         ;
	/** Forwarded principal (omitted for the host's own principal). */
	         principal                       ;
	         owner           ;
	         address        ;
	         proxy        ;
	         ready               ;
	status               = "loading";
	error         ;
	shape                   ;
	binding                                                                      ;
	         shapeListeners = new Set                        ();
	         #readables = new Map                        ();
	         #callers = new Map                                                        ();
	#settleReady                                                                       ;
	#live                                                                                   ;

	constructor(
		host            ,
		fields   
			                      
			           
			               
			                                 
			                 
			                
		 ,
	) {
		this.host = host;
		this.contract = fields.contract;
		this.at = fields.at;
		this.input = fields.input;
		this.principal = fields.principal;
		this.owner = fields.owner;
		this.address = fields.address;
		this.ready = new Promise      ((resolve, reject) => {
			this.#settleReady = { resolve, reject };
		});
		this.ready.catch(remoteNoop);
		this.#live = this.#newLive();
		this.proxy = new Proxy(this, {
			get: (target, property) => {
				if (HANDLE_BASE.has(property)) {
					const value          = Reflect.get(target, property, target);
					return typeof value === "function" ? value.bind(target) : value;
				}
				if (typeof property !== "string" || property === "then") return undefined;
				return target.member(property);
			},
		});
		holdsByProxy.set(this.proxy, this);
	}

	get kind()                     {
		return this.shape?.kind;
	}

	onShape(listener                        )             {
		this.shapeListeners.add(listener);
		return () => this.shapeListeners.delete(listener);
	}

	onStatus(listener                )             {
		this.#statusListeners.add(listener);
		return () => this.#statusListeners.delete(listener);
	}

	         #statusListeners = new Set                ();

	#statusChanged()       {
		notifyStatus(this.#statusListeners, this.status, this.error, (error) => this.host.report(error));
	}

	release()       {
		if (this.status === "released" || this.status === "gone") return;
		this.status = "released";
		this.#statusChanged();
		const error = new ChordError("released", `handle ${this.address} released`);
		this.#settleReady?.reject(error);
		this.#live.reject(error);
		for (const readable of this.#readables.values()) readable.stop();
		if (this.binding !== undefined) this.binding.channel.release(this.binding.id);
		this.binding = undefined;
		this.host.forgetRemote(this);
	}

	// ─── driven by the link ───

	bound(channel               , id        )       {
		this.binding = { channel, id };
	}

	shaped(shape       )       {
		if (this.status === "released" || this.status === "gone") return;
		this.shape = shape;
		const wasLoading = this.status === "loading";
		this.status = "live";
		if (wasLoading) this.#statusChanged();
		if (wasLoading) {
			this.#settleReady?.resolve();
			this.#settleReady = undefined;
			this.#live.resolve();
		}
		for (const readable of this.#readables.values()) readable.bind();
		for (const listener of [...this.shapeListeners]) {
			try {
				listener(shape);
			} catch (error) {
				this.host.report(error);
			}
		}
	}

	gone(error         )       {
		if (this.status === "released" || this.status === "gone") return;
		this.status = "gone";
		this.error = error;
		this.#statusChanged();
		this.binding = undefined;
		this.#settleReady?.reject(error);
		this.#live.reject(error);
		for (const readable of this.#readables.values()) readable.stop();
		this.host.forgetRemote(this);
	}

	/** The link went down but may come back (upstream reconnect): wait, then re-hold. */
	detached()       {
		if (this.status === "released" || this.status === "gone") return;
		this.binding = undefined;
		for (const readable of this.#readables.values()) readable.detach();
		if (this.status === "live") {
			this.status = "loading";
			this.#live = this.#newLive();
			this.#statusChanged();
		}
	}

	// ─── members ───

	member(name        )          {
		const description = Object.values(this.contract.description.variants)
			.map((members) => members[name])
			.find((member) => member !== undefined);
		if (description?.kind === "method") return this.#caller(name);
		if (description?.kind === "state") {
			let readable = this.#readables.get(name);
			if (readable === undefined) {
				readable = new RemoteReadable(this, name);
				this.#readables.set(name, readable);
			}
			return readable;
		}
		return undefined;
	}

	#caller(name        )                                                 {
		let caller = this.#callers.get(name);
		if (caller === undefined) {
			caller = (args, ctx) => {
				if (this.host.inFactory > 0)
					throw new ChordError("not_ready", `${this.address}.${name} called inside a factory`);
				if (ctx instanceof CtxImpl) {
					try {
						ctx.check(ctx.host);
					} catch (error) {
						return Promise.reject(error);
					}
				}
				if (ctx === undefined || (ctx instanceof CtxImpl && ctx.holder)) {
					return this.host.root((root) => this.#call(name, args, root), { signal: ctx?.signal });
				}
				return this.#call(name, args, ctx);
			};
			this.#callers.set(name, caller);
		}
		return caller;
	}

	async #call(name        , args         , ctx     )                   {
		for (;;) {
			if (this.status === "released" || this.status === "gone") {
				throw this.status === "gone" ? this.error : new ChordError("released", `handle ${this.address} released`);
			}
			if (this.status === "loading") await raceSignal(this.#live.promise, ctx.signal);
			const binding = this.binding;
			if (binding === undefined) continue;
			const kind = this.shape?.kind;
			if (kind === undefined || this.contract.description.variants[kind]?.[name]?.kind !== "method") {
				throw new ChordError("not_in_variant", `${this.address}: ${name} is not a method of variant ${kind}`);
			}
			// Send only what the consumer's own schema knows (the additive rule's premise; see RecordImpl.call).
			const description = this.contract.description.variants[kind]?.[name];
			const sent = description?.kind === "method" ? Value.Clean(description.args, Value.Clone(args)) : args;
			return binding.channel.call(binding.id, name, sent, { rootId: ctx.rootId, epoch: ctx.epoch }, ctx.signal);
		}
	}

	#newLive()                                                                                    {
		let resolve = remoteNoop;
		let reject                           = remoteNoop;
		const promise = new Promise      ((done, fail) => {
			resolve = done;
			reject = fail;
		});
		promise.catch(remoteNoop);
		return { promise, resolve, reject };
	}
}

// ══ host.ts ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
/** The principal of in-host code. */
const hostPrincipal            = Object.freeze({ host: true });

/**
 * Registers a factory for a type. Overloaded on the contract's definition (not the contract type) so TypeScript
 * keeps literal `kind`s in factory returns.
 */
                        
	                      
		                        
		                                    
		                                          
	        
	                     
		                       
		                                   
		                                         
	        
 

                                                                                                   

/**
 * What a facet's `setup` gets. `serve` registers types; `root` and `registry` give the facet's code that is not a
 * Chord method, listener or task (Durable tools and hooks, callbacks of native libraries) access to its own host,
 * so plugins never need a process-global host. Both may be kept and used after `setup` returns.
 */
                           
	                      
	                                                     
	                      
	                                                                             
	                                                                             
	                                                                                         
	                                                    
 

/** What a facet module exports (default export): the code that serves its types on one host kind. */
                                  
	                                                                                
	                                       
	                           
 

/** A named facet. Modules are named by their URL; reloading a facet replaces everything it served. */
                                                
	                      
 

const defineFacet = (
	setup                         ,
	options                                             = {},
)                  => ({ setup, uses: options.uses });

/** Imports facet modules (fresh copies on every call) and names them by module URL. */
                                                                                   

/** A facet object, or a module (path or URL) whose default export is a FacetDefinition. */
                                             

const isFacet = (ref           )               => typeof ref === "object" && !(ref instanceof URL);

                              
	                       
	                                                                                                    
	                                                         
	                                                                              
	                       
	                                                                                           
	                                            
	                                                                                    
	                              
	                                                                                                     
	                                  
	                                                                                     
	                         
	                                       
 

/** Where requests for hosts named `prefix…` go (on the server). */
                        
	                                                                                           
	                                                                              
	                                             
	                                         
	                                                                                             
	                                                     
 

/** `invalidatePrincipal` matches: a predicate (this host only), or a pattern (fields that must be equal; propagates). */
                                                                                                     

                              
	                               
	                              
	                                                                                              
	                        
 

                              
	                                                                                                     
	                              
 

                       
	                      
	                       
	                                           
	                      
	                                                                                                   
	                                                            
	                                                                                       
	                                                                          
	                                                                                                         
	                       
	                                                                                        
	                                                                             
	                                                    
	   
	                                                                                                               
	                                                        
	   
	                                                          
	                                                                                               
	                                                 
	                                                                                                            
	                                                 
	                                          
	                                                                                                    
	                                                                                      
	                       
 

                             
	                    
	                                        
	                                                    
	                                                                 
	                                    
 

const EMPTY_TABLE                       = new Map               ();

function createHost(options              = {})       {
	return new HostImpl(options);
}

class HostImpl                           {
	         name        ;
	         dev         ;
	         principal            = hostPrincipal;
	inFactory = 0;
	failed         ;
	         records = new Map                    ();
	         epochs = new Map                    ();
	         facets = new Map               ();
	         eager = new Map              ();
	         kind                   ;
	         connections = new Set            ();
	upstream                        ;
	/** Remote handles this host holds (re-held after an upstream reconnect). */
	         remotes = new Set              ();
	         #routes = new Map               ();
	         #childReloads = new ChildReloads(this);
	/** Child facet modules by host-name prefix, from every committed cluster reload (for reconciling children). */
	remoteModules                           = {};
	/** Removal tombstones also reconcile children that connected during a transaction. */
	remoteRemoved                           = {};
	/** Highest epoch the parent has retired. */
	#retireAllowed = 0;
	/** Roots that came over a link, by the link they came from. */
	/** Foreign roots (started on another host), by the links they came over. */
	         #foreign = new Map                         ();
	/** Roots started on this host that have not ended. */
	         #local = new Set        ();
	         #incarnation = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);
	/** Links each root's calls went out on. */
	         #rootLinks = new Map                         ();
	current            ;
	previous                        ;
	/** The epoch a running reload will commit; new types activated during prepare install their behavior for it. */
	pending                        ;
	/** Activations of these types wait (reload gate). */
	/** Types behind a reload gate: new records of these types are rejected `upgrade_conflict` until commit/abort. */
	gating                                 ;
	reloading = false;
	retired             = () => {};
	         #principalKey                                  ;
	         #onError                          ;
	         #loader                         ;
	         #dying = new Map                    ();
	         #touches = new Map                         ();
	         #order = new Map                             ();
	         #subscribers = new Map                                                                     ();
	#epochSeq = 1;
	#rootSeq = 0;

	constructor(options             ) {
		this.name = options.name ?? "host";
		this.dev = options.dev ?? false;
		this.#principalKey = options.principalKey ?? canonicalJson;
		this.#onError = options.onError ?? ((error) => console.error("chord:", error));
		this.#loader = options.loader;
		this.current = { id: this.#epochSeq, services: new Map(), tables: new Map(), roots: new Map() };
		this.epochs.set(this.current.id, this.current);
		this.kind = options.kind ?? "host";
		if (options.upstream !== undefined) this.connect(options.upstream, options.upstreamOptions);
	}

	get epoch()         {
		return this.current.id;
	}

	get previousEpoch()                     {
		return this.previous?.id;
	}

	/** Allocates the next epoch id, or adopts one issued by the coordinating server. */
	nextEpochId(given         )         {
		if (given !== undefined && given <= this.current.id)
			throw new Error(`epoch ${given} is not newer than ${this.current.id}`);
		this.#epochSeq = given ?? this.#epochSeq + 1;
		return this.#epochSeq;
	}

	report(error         )       {
		this.#onError(error);
	}

	// ─── serving ───

	         serve        = ((type, factory, options = {}) => {
		if (this.reloading) throw new Error("host.serve during a reload; serve from a facet passed to reload()");
		this.serveInto(this.current.services, makeService(type, factory, options, ""));
		if (options.eager) this.startEager(this.current.services.get(type.name) );
	})                            ;

	install(facet       , options              = {})                {
		if (this.reloading) throw new Error("host.install during a reload");
		if (this.facets.has(facet.name)) throw new Error(`facet ${facet.name} is already installed; use reload()`);
		const added            = [];
		facet.setup(
			this.facetApi(((type, factory, options = {}) => {
				const service = makeService(type, factory, options, facet.name);
				this.serveInto(this.current.services, service);
				added.push(service);
			})                            ),
		);
		this.facets.set(facet.name, facet);
		const holds = added.filter((service) => service.options.eager).map((service) => this.startEager(service));
		return options.awaitEager === false ? Promise.resolve() : this.#eagerReady(holds);
	}

	async load(plugins                      , options              = {})                {
		const facets = await this.resolve(plugins);
		// No await between installs: an eager factory may consume a type served by a later facet.
		await Promise.all(facets.map((facet) => this.install(facet, options)));
	}

	ready()                {
		return this.#eagerReady(this.eager.values());
	}

	#eagerReady(holds                )                {
		const ready = Promise.all([...holds].map((hold) => hold.ready)).then(() => {});
		// Ignoring install's return still reports through onError without an unhandled rejection.
		ready.catch(() => {});
		return ready;
	}

	/** Turns plugin references into facets, importing modules through the loader. */
	async resolve(plugins                      )                   {
		const modules = plugins.filter((ref)                      => !isFacet(ref));
		if (modules.length > 0 && this.#loader === undefined) {
			throw new Error(
				"loading modules needs createHost({ loader }), e.g. nodeLoader from @earendil-works/chord/node",
			);
		}
		const loaded = modules.length > 0 && this.#loader !== undefined ? await this.#loader(modules) : [];
		return plugins.map((ref) => (isFacet(ref) ? ref : loaded.shift() ));
	}

	facetApi(serve       )           {
		return {
			serve,
			name: this.name,
			root: (fn, options) => this.root(fn, options),
			registry: (registry) => this.registry(registry),
		};
	}

	serveInto(services                      , service         )       {
		if (services.has(service.contract.name)) throw new Error(`${service.contract.name} is already served`);
		services.set(service.contract.name, service);
	}

	startEager(service         )       {
		const eager = service.options.eager;
		const input = typeof eager === "object" ? eager.input : {};
		const handle = this.acquire(service.contract, input, undefined, "host");
		this.eager.set(service.contract.name, handle);
		handle.ready.catch((error         ) => this.report(error));
		return handle;
	}

	// ─── HostCore ───

	currentEpoch()         {
		return this.current.id;
	}

	hasEpoch(epoch        )          {
		return this.epochs.has(epoch);
	}

	*retainedEpochs()                                                            {
		for (const [id, state] of this.epochs) yield [id, state.services];
		if (this.pending !== undefined) yield [this.pending.id, this.pending.services];
	}

	pendingEpoch()                     {
		return this.pending?.id;
	}

	/** The current code for a type; a type a running reload adds is served from the pending epoch. */
	service(typeName        )                      {
		return this.current.services.get(typeName) ?? this.pending?.services.get(typeName);
	}

	addressOf(type             , input         , principal           , at         , candidate = false)         {
		if (at !== undefined && at !== this.name)
			return remoteAddress(at, type, input, principal === this.principal ? undefined : principal);
		const service = this.#bindingService(type.name, candidate);
		if (service === undefined) throw unknownType(type.name, this.name);
		const normalized = normalize(service.contract.input, input, `${type.name} input`);
		return this.#address(service, normalized, principal);
	}

	#address(service         , normalized         , principal           )         {
		const key = principal === this.principal ? "host" : `p:${this.#principalKey(principal)}`;
		return `${service.contract.name}\u0000${canonicalIdentity(service.contract.input, normalized)}\u0000${key}`;
	}

	acquire(
		type             ,
		input         ,
		options                        ,
		owner           ,
		from             ,
		candidate = false,
	)       {
		if (options?.at !== undefined && options.at !== this.name)
			return this.#acquireRemote(type, input, options, owner);
		const service = this.#bindingService(type.name, candidate);
		if (service === undefined) throw unknownType(type.name, this.name);
		bind(service.contract, service.options.accepts, type);
		const principal = options?.principal ?? this.principal;
		const normalized = normalize(service.contract.input, input, `${type.name} input`);
		const address = this.#address(service, normalized, principal);
		let record = this.records.get(address);
		if (from !== undefined && record !== undefined && (record === from || reaches(record, from))) {
			throw invalid("cycle", `${from.address} -> ${address} would create a hold cycle`);
		}
		if (record === undefined) {
			if (this.gating?.has(type.name)) {
				throw new ChordError("upgrade_conflict", `${type.name} is being upgraded; retry`, { reason: "gate" });
			}
			const dying = this.#dying.get(address);
			if (dying?.status === "disposed") this.#dying.delete(address);
			record = new RecordImpl(this, {
				address,
				typeName: type.name,
				input: normalized,
				principal,
				eager:
					service.options.eager !== undefined && service.options.eager !== false && principal === this.principal,
			});
			this.records.set(address, record);
			// A new record for an address still being disposed waits for that cleanup (exclusive resources).
			record.start(dying?.status === "disposing" ? dying.dispose(false) : undefined);
		}
		const handle = new HandleImpl(record, type, owner);
		record.addHold(handle);
		return handle;
	}

	forget(record            )       {
		if (this.records.get(record.address) === record) this.records.delete(record.address);
		if (record.status === "disposing") this.#dying.set(record.address, record);
	}

	touch(rootId        , record            )       {
		let touched = this.#touches.get(rootId);
		if (touched === undefined) {
			touched = new Set();
			this.#touches.set(rootId, touched);
		}
		touched.add(record);
	}

	// ─── roots ───

	async root   (fn                              , options              = {})             {
		// The incarnation keeps root ids unique across restarts of a host with the same name.
		const rootId = `${this.name}#${this.#incarnation}:${++this.#rootSeq}`;
		this.#local.add(rootId);
		return this.joinRoot(rootId, options.epoch ?? this.current.id, fn, options);
	}

	/**
	 * Runs `fn` inside root `rootId` of `epoch`. A root counts as running here (for retirement) while any of its calls
	 * runs here. A root that started here ends when its last call here settles, even if it went out and came back
	 * over a link meanwhile. A foreign root (started elsewhere, arriving `from` a link) keeps its leases on records
	 * until every link it came over announced its end (`root_end`) or closed, so its re-entry stays grandfathered.
	 */
	async joinRoot   (
		rootId        ,
		epochId        ,
		fn                              ,
		options                                                                                                = {},
	)             {
		const epoch = this.epochs.get(epochId);
		if (epoch === undefined) {
			this.#local.delete(rootId);
			throw new ChordError("superseded", `epoch ${epochId} is not retained by ${this.name}`);
		}
		if (options.from !== undefined && !this.#local.has(rootId)) {
			let links = this.#foreign.get(rootId);
			if (links === undefined) {
				links = new Set();
				this.#foreign.set(rootId, links);
			}
			links.add(options.from);
		}
		epoch.roots.set(rootId, (epoch.roots.get(rootId) ?? 0) + 1);
		const ctx = new CtxImpl(this, {
			signal: options.signal ?? new AbortController().signal,
			rootId,
			epoch: epoch.id,
			principal: options.principal ?? this.principal,
		});
		try {
			return await fn(ctx);
		} finally {
			ctx.close();
			const count = (epoch.roots.get(rootId) ?? 1) - 1;
			if (count > 0) epoch.roots.set(rootId, count);
			else {
				epoch.roots.delete(rootId);
				if (!this.#foreign.has(rootId)) this.endRoot(rootId);
				this.maybeRetire();
			}
		}
	}

	/** Remembers that calls of `rootId` went out on `connection`, which must hear when the root ends. */
	rootSent(rootId        , connection            )       {
		let links = this.#rootLinks.get(rootId);
		if (links === undefined) {
			links = new Set();
			this.#rootLinks.set(rootId, links);
		}
		links.add(connection);
	}

	/** A root ended (here, or announced by its origin): end its leases and tell the links it went out on. */
	endRoot(rootId        )       {
		this.#local.delete(rootId);
		this.#foreign.delete(rootId);
		for (const record of this.#touches.get(rootId) ?? []) record.rootEnded(rootId);
		this.#touches.delete(rootId);
		for (const connection of this.#rootLinks.get(rootId) ?? []) connection.send({ t: "root_end", rootId });
		this.#rootLinks.delete(rootId);
	}

	/**
	 * `from` announced the end of a foreign root (or closed). Honored only if the root came over `from`: a peer cannot
	 * end leases of roots it never carried. The root ends when no link that brought it remains.
	 */
	foreignRootEnded(rootId        , from            )       {
		const links = this.#foreign.get(rootId);
		if (links === undefined || !links.delete(from) || links.size > 0) return;
		const running = [...this.epochs.values()].some((epoch) => epoch.roots.has(rootId));
		// Still running here (a call in flight): the lease ends when it settles.
		if (running) this.#foreign.delete(rootId);
		else this.endRoot(rootId);
	}

	/**
	 * Retires the previous epoch once no local roots of it remain. A child also waits for its parent to retire it:
	 * roots span hosts, and an old root on the server may still call in.
	 */
	maybeRetire()       {
		const previous = this.previous;
		if (previous === undefined || previous.roots.size > 0) return;
		if (this.kind !== "client" && this.upstream !== undefined && previous.id > this.#retireAllowed) return;
		this.retire();
	}

	allowRetire(epoch        )       {
		this.#retireAllowed = Math.max(this.#retireAllowed, epoch);
		this.maybeRetire();
	}

	/** Drops the previous epoch: its behaviors and the declarations only it used. */
	retire()       {
		const previous = this.previous;
		if (previous === undefined) return;
		this.previous = undefined;
		this.epochs.delete(previous.id);
		for (const record of [...this.records.values()]) this.retireBehavior(record, previous.id);
		for (const connection of this.connections) {
			if (connection.role === "downstream" && connection.peer?.kind === "host")
				connection.send({ t: "retire", epoch: previous.id });
		}
		this.retired();
	}

	/**
	 * Drops a record's behavior for `epoch` and what only that behavior declared, in disposal order: resources close
	 * (reverse open order) before its holds are released.
	 */
	retireBehavior(record            , epoch        )       {
		const installed = record.behaviors.get(epoch);
		record.behaviors.delete(epoch);
		if (installed === undefined || installed.decls === record.decls) return;
		const kept = record.allDecls();
		const keep =    (pick                               )         =>
			new Set([...kept].flatMap((decls) => [...pick(decls)]));
		const holds = keep((decls) => decls.holds.values());
		const resources = keep((decls) => decls.resources.values());
		const derives = keep((decls) => decls.derives.values());
		for (const derived of installed.decls.derives.values()) if (!derives.has(derived)) derived.detach();
		const closing = [...installed.decls.resources.values()]
			.filter((resource) => !resources.has(resource))
			.sort((a, b) => record.openOrder.indexOf(b) - record.openOrder.indexOf(a));
		const releasing = [...installed.decls.holds.values()].filter((hold) => !holds.has(hold));
		record.refreshSchemas();
		runAll(
			[
				...closing.map((resource) => () => resource.close(record.openOrder)),
				...releasing.map((hold) => () => hold.release()),
			],
			`${record.address}: retiring epoch ${epoch} failed`,
		).catch((error         ) => this.report(error));
	}

	// ─── registries ───

	registry   (registry             )                  {
		const table = (ctx      )                   => {
			const epoch = ctx === undefined ? this.current : this.epochs.get(ctx.epoch);
			if (epoch === undefined) throw new ChordError("superseded", `epoch ${ctx?.epoch} is retired`);
			return (epoch.tables.get(registry.name) ?? EMPTY_TABLE)                    ;
		};
		return {
			table,
			get: (key, ctx) => table(ctx).get(key),
			subscribe: (listener) => {
				let set = this.#subscribers.get(registry.name);
				if (set === undefined) {
					set = new Set();
					this.#subscribers.set(registry.name, set);
				}
				const wrapped = listener                                                          ;
				set.add(wrapped);
				wrapped(table(), this.current.id);
				return () => set.delete(wrapped);
			},
		};
	}

	/** Builds registry tables from (record, declarations) pairs. Throws registry_conflict. */
	buildTables(entries                                        )                                      {
		const byRegistry = new Map                                                             ();
		for (const [record, decls] of entries) {
			for (const { registry, key, value } of decls.registrations.values()) {
				let entries = byRegistry.get(registry.name);
				if (entries === undefined) {
					entries = new Map();
					byRegistry.set(registry.name, entries);
				}
				const existing = entries.get(key);
				if (existing !== undefined && existing.record !== record && registry.conflict === "error") {
					throw invalid(
						"registry_conflict",
						`${registry.name}: ${key} registered by ${existing.record.address} and ${record.address}`,
					);
				}
				entries.set(key, { value, record });
				let order = this.#order.get(registry.name);
				if (order === undefined) {
					order = new Map();
					this.#order.set(registry.name, order);
				}
				if (!order.has(key)) order.set(key, order.size);
			}
		}
		const tables = new Map                                ();
		for (const [name, entries] of byRegistry) {
			const order = this.#order.get(name) ;
			const sorted = [...entries].sort(([a], [b]) => order.get(a)  - order.get(b) );
			tables.set(name, new Map(sorted.map(([key, entry]) => [key, entry.value])));
		}
		return tables;
	}

	/**
	 * The service a hold binds against: the current one, or for a prepare rerun (`candidate`) the running reload's
	 * pending one, so consumer and provider of a breaking change can reload in one batch.
	 */
	#bindingService(typeName        , candidate         )                      {
		return (candidate ? this.pending?.services.get(typeName) : undefined) ?? this.service(typeName);
	}

	/** Live records with their current declarations; records waiting for a reload's commit only with `candidates`. */
	liveDecls(candidates = false)                        {
		return [...this.records.values()]
			.filter((record) => record.status === "live" && (candidates || !record.deferredRuntime))
			.map((record) => [record, record.decls]);
	}

	checkRegistrations(record            , decls       )       {
		if (decls.registrations.size === 0) return;
		this.buildTables([...this.liveDecls().filter(([other]) => other !== record), [record, decls]]);
	}

	registriesChanged(including             )       {
		const entries = this.liveDecls().filter(([record]) => record !== including);
		if (including !== undefined) entries.push([including, including.decls]);
		this.installTables(this.current, this.buildTables(entries));
	}

	/** A commit failed midway: from now on this host admits no work (calls fail `unavailable/host_failed`). */
	fail(error         )       {
		this.failed ??= error;
		this.report(error);
	}

	/** Installs tables for `epoch`; subscribers hear about changed tables of the current epoch (all tables when `all`). */
	installTables(epoch            , tables                                     , all = false)       {
		const before = epoch.tables;
		epoch.tables = tables;
		if (epoch !== this.current) return;
		for (const [name, listeners] of this.#subscribers) {
			const next = tables.get(name) ?? EMPTY_TABLE;
			if (!all && sameTable(before.get(name) ?? EMPTY_TABLE, next)) continue;
			const current = [...listeners];
			enqueue(() => {
				for (const listener of current) {
					try {
						listener(next, epoch.id);
					} catch (error) {
						this.report(error);
					}
				}
			});
		}
	}

	// ─── public API ───

	get                       (type   , input            , options             )            {
		return this.acquire(type, input, options, "external").proxy             ;
	}

	async invalidatePrincipal(match                )                {
		const matches = typeof match === "function" ? match : (principal           ) => matchesPattern(match, principal);
		const reason = new ChordError("gone", "principal invalidated");
		// Links acting as a matching principal are re-stamped (if the acceptor can) or closed.
		for (const connection of [...this.connections]) {
			if (connection.role !== "downstream" || !matches(connection.principal)) continue;
			// Relays under the old principal close at once, including those still resolving their route.
			connection.revoke(connection.restamp?.(connection.principal));
		}
		// Patterns propagate to child hosts, which dispose their own matching records (e.g. relayed guests).
		if (typeof match !== "function") {
			for (const connection of this.connections) {
				if (connection.role === "downstream" && connection.peer.kind === "host") {
					connection.send({ t: "invalidate", pattern: match });
				}
			}
		}
		const matching = [...this.records.values()].filter(
			(record) => record.principal !== this.principal && matches(record.principal),
		);
		await disposeHoldersFirst(matching, reason, "invalidatePrincipal: disposal failed");
	}

	// ─── links ───

	accept(link      , options               )       {
		const kind = options.kind ?? "client";
		if (kind === "host" && options.name === undefined) throw new TypeError("accepting a host link needs its name");
		this.#addConnection(link, {
			...options,
			role: "downstream",
			principal: options.principal,
			peer: { kind, name: kind === "host" ? options.name : undefined },
		});
	}

	connect(link      , options              = {})       {
		if (this.upstream?.open) this.upstream.link.close("replaced");
		const connection = this.#addConnection(link, {
			...options,
			role: "upstream",
			principal: undefined,
			peer: { kind: "host", name: undefined },
		});
		this.upstream = connection;
		// Remote handles that lost their link re-hold over the new one; state subscribers get a reset.
		for (const handle of this.remotes) if (handle.binding === undefined) connection.hold(handle);
	}

	#addConnection(link      , config                                             )             {
		const connection = new Connection(this, link, config);
		// A link that was already closed closed the connection in its constructor: keeping it would route to it forever.
		if (connection.open) this.connections.add(connection);
		return connection;
	}

	route(prefix        , route       )       {
		this.#routes.set(prefix, route);
	}

	/**
	 * A child host announced its epoch. In step: usable. Behind: fenced (nothing is routed to it) and reconciled by a
	 * reload of its modules to this host's epoch, never by renumbering what it runs. Ahead: impossible, refused.
	 */
	peerHello(connection            , epoch        )       {
		if (epoch === this.current.id) {
			connection.markSynced(epoch);
			return;
		}
		if (epoch > this.current.id) {
			this.report(new ChordError("invalid", `${connection.name} runs epoch ${epoch}, ahead of ${this.current.id}`));
			connection.link.close("epoch ahead of parent");
			return;
		}
		connection.peerEpoch = epoch;
		connection.fencedEpochs.add(epoch);
		reconcileChild(this, connection).catch((error         ) => {
			this.report(error);
			connection.link.close("reconciliation failed");
		});
	}

	connectionClosed(connection            )       {
		this.connections.delete(connection);
		// Foreign roots that came over this link will never announce their end over it.
		for (const [rootId, links] of [...this.#foreign])
			if (links.has(connection)) this.foreignRootEnded(rootId, connection);
		for (const links of this.#rootLinks.values()) links.delete(connection);
		if (this.upstream === connection) {
			this.upstream = undefined;
			this.#childReloads.parentLost();
		}
	}

	forgetRemote(handle              )       {
		this.remotes.delete(handle);
	}

	/** A downstream host connection by its configured name (never by what a peer announced). */
	#childNamed(name        )                         {
		return [...this.connections].find(
			(connection) =>
				connection.open &&
				connection.role === "downstream" &&
				connection.peer.kind === "host" &&
				connection.peer.name === name,
		);
	}

	#acquireRemote(type             , input         , options            , owner           )       {
		const at = options.at ;
		if (type.scope !== "remote") throw invalid("local", `${type.name} is local and cannot be reached at ${at}`);
		const principal =
			options.principal === undefined || options.principal === this.principal ? undefined : options.principal;
		const handle = new RemoteHandle(this, {
			contract: type,
			at,
			input,
			principal,
			owner,
			address: remoteAddress(at, type, input, principal),
		});
		this.remotes.add(handle);
		const bindWhenSynced = (connection                     )       => {
			connection.then(
				(target) => {
					if (handle.status === "loading" && handle.binding === undefined) target.hold(handle);
				},
				(error         ) => handle.gone(error),
			);
		};
		const direct = this.#childNamed(at);
		if (this.upstream !== undefined) this.upstream.hold(handle);
		else if (direct !== undefined) bindWhenSynced(direct.whenSynced().then(() => direct));
		else {
			const route = this.#routeFor(at);
			if (route === undefined) handle.gone(new ChordError("unavailable", `no route to ${at}`));
			else bindWhenSynced(this.#resolve(route, at, principal ?? this.principal));
		}
		return handle;
	}

	#routeFor(hostName        )                    {
		let best                             ;
		for (const entry of this.#routes) {
			if (hostName.startsWith(entry[0]) && (best === undefined || entry[0].length > best[0].length)) best = entry;
		}
		return best?.[1];
	}

	/** Authorize, resolve (maybe spawn), authorize again; returns the synced connection to the host. */
	async #resolve(
		route       ,
		hostName        ,
		principal           ,
		still                = () => true,
	)                      {
		const refuse = ()             => new ChordError("forbidden", `${hostName} refused for this principal`);
		const revoked = ()             => new ChordError("gone", "principal invalidated while resolving");
		if (!(await route.authorize(principal, hostName))) throw refuse();
		if (!still()) throw revoked();
		const link = await route.resolve(hostName);
		if (!still()) throw revoked();
		if (!(await route.authorize(principal, hostName))) throw refuse();
		if (!still()) throw revoked();
		let connection = [...this.connections].find((candidate) => candidate.link === link);
		connection ??= this.#addConnection(link, {
			role: "downstream",
			principal: route.principal?.(hostName) ?? { host: hostName },
			peer: { kind: "host", name: hostName },
		});
		await connection.whenSynced();
		if (!still()) throw revoked();
		return connection;
	}

	/**
	 * Relays a hold from `relay.origin` to the host it names. Relaying needs a route: it is where authorization
	 * lives. The origin's authorization generation is re-checked after every await and right before forwarding, so a
	 * revocation during resolution wins.
	 */
	async relay(relay           )                {
		const at = relay.frame.at;
		const route = this.#routeFor(at);
		if (route === undefined) {
			throw this.#childNamed(at) === undefined
				? new ChordError("unavailable", `no route to ${at}`)
				: new ChordError("forbidden", `no route authorizes ${at}`);
		}
		const down = await this.#resolve(route, at, relay.principal, () => relay.current);
		if (!relay.current) return;
		relay.open(down);
	}

	reload(plugins                      , options                = {})                        {
		// Client-local facet epochs never cross the parent link; only child hosts require cluster coordination.
		if (this.kind !== "client" && this.upstream !== undefined) {
			return Promise.reject(
				new ChordError("unavailable", "reloads are coordinated by the server", { reason: "not_coordinator" }),
			);
		}
		const children = [...this.connections].some(
			(connection) => connection.role === "downstream" && connection.peer.kind === "host",
		);
		return children || options.remote !== undefined || options.remoteRemove !== undefined
			? runClusterReload(this, plugins, options)
			: runReload(this, plugins, options);
	}

	onReloadFrame(connection            , frame                                                       )       {
		this.#childReloads.handle(connection, frame);
	}

	rememberRemoteModules(
		remote                                                         ,
		remove                                                         ,
	)       {
		for (const prefix of new Set([...Object.keys(remote ?? {}), ...Object.keys(remove ?? {})])) {
			const modules = remote?.[prefix] ?? [];
			const removed = remove?.[prefix] ?? [];
			this.remoteModules[prefix] = [...new Set([...(this.remoteModules[prefix] ?? []), ...modules])].filter(
				(module) => !removed.includes(module),
			);
			this.remoteRemoved[prefix] = [...new Set([...(this.remoteRemoved[prefix] ?? []), ...removed])].filter(
				(module) => !modules.includes(module),
			);
		}
	}

	/** The epoch id the next reload would commit. */
	peekNextEpoch()         {
		return this.#epochSeq + 1;
	}

	/**
	 * Disposes every record, holders before what they hold (a resource's `close` may still use the objects its record
	 * holds, local or remote), then closes the links.
	 */
	async close()                {
		this.eager.clear();
		const reason = new ChordError("gone", `host ${this.name} closed`);
		try {
			await disposeHoldersFirst([...this.records.values()], reason, "host close: disposal failed");
		} finally {
			for (const connection of [...this.connections]) connection.link.close("host closed");
		}
	}
}

/**
 * Force-disposes `records` in waves: a record goes once no record of the set that holds it is left, so holders are
 * disposed (and their resources closed) while what they hold is still live. In-host holds are acyclic. Errors of
 * every disposal are collected.
 */
async function disposeHoldersFirst(records                       , reason         , message        )                {
	const remaining = new Set(records);
	const held = (record            )                  => {
		const result = new Set            ();
		for (const decls of record.allDecls()) {
			for (const hold of decls.holds.values()) if (hold.record !== undefined) result.add(hold.record);
		}
		return result;
	};
	const holds = new Map(records.map((record) => [record, held(record)]         ));
	const errors            = [];
	while (remaining.size > 0) {
		const heldByRemaining = new Set            ();
		for (const record of remaining) for (const target of holds.get(record) ?? []) heldByRemaining.add(target);
		let wave = [...remaining].filter((record) => !heldByRemaining.has(record));
		// A cycle cannot happen in-host; dispose the rest together rather than loop.
		if (wave.length === 0) wave = [...remaining];
		for (const record of wave) remaining.delete(record);
		const results = await Promise.allSettled(wave.map((record) => record.dispose(true, reason)));
		for (const result of results) if (result.status === "rejected") errors.push(result.reason);
	}
	if (errors.length === 1) throw errors[0];
	if (errors.length > 1) throw new AggregateError(errors, message);
}

function sameTable(a                        , b                        )          {
	if (a.size !== b.size) return false;
	const keys = [...a.keys()];
	return [...b].every(([key, value], index) => keys[index] === key && a.get(key) === value);
}

const matchesPattern = (pattern                                   , principal           )          =>
	principal !== null &&
	typeof principal === "object" &&
	Object.entries(pattern).every(([key, value]) => deepEqual((principal                           )[key], value));

/** Address of a remote hold, as the consumer sees it (the provider normalizes the input). */
const remoteAddress = (at        , type             , input         , principal                       )         =>
	`@${at}\u0000${type.name}\u0000${canonicalJson(input)}\u0000${principal === undefined ? "host" : canonicalJson(principal)}`;

const unknownType = (type        , host        )             =>
	new ChordError("unavailable", `${type} is not served by ${host}`, { reason: "unknown_type" });

/** True if `from` holds `to`, directly or transitively, through record-owned holds on this host. */
function reaches(from            , to            )          {
	const seen = new Set            ();
	const stack = [from];
	while (stack.length > 0) {
		const record = stack.pop() ;
		if (record === to) return true;
		if (seen.has(record)) continue;
		seen.add(record);
		// Remote holds have no record here: cycles across hosts are avoided by convention.
		for (const decls of record.allDecls()) {
			for (const hold of decls.holds.values()) if (hold.record !== undefined) stack.push(hold.record);
		}
	}
	return false;
}

// ══ registry.ts ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
/** `conflict: "error"` rejects two records registering one key; `"last"` keeps the later registration. */
const defineRegistry =    (name        , options                                  = {})              => ({
	name,
	conflict: options.conflict ?? "error",
});

// ══ context/index.ts ════════════════════════════════════════════════════
/** Typed identity for one value carried by a {@link Context}. */
                                
	                       
	                                                                                                 
	                                     
 

/** Immutable invocation-scoped values passed explicitly through operations. */
                          
	                                              
	                                            
	                   
 

const ABORT_SIGNAL_CONTEXT_KEY                                      = Object.freeze({
	token: Symbol("chord.abortSignal"),
});

         class BaseContext                    {
	                                                     
	                            

	get abortSignal()                          {
		return this.value(ABORT_SIGNAL_CONTEXT_KEY);
	}
}

class EmptyContext extends BaseContext {
	         #name        ;

	constructor(name        ) {
		super();
		this.#name = name;
	}

	value   (_key               )                {
		return undefined;
	}

	toString()         {
		return this.#name;
	}
}

class ContextValue    extends BaseContext {
	         #parent         ;
	         #key               ;
	         #value   ;

	constructor(parent         , key               , value   ) {
		super();
		this.#parent = parent;
		this.#key = key;
		this.#value = value;
	}

	value       (key                   )                    {
		if (key.token === this.#key.token) return this.#value                    ;
		return this.#parent.value(key);
	}

	toString()         {
		return `${this.#parent}.WithValue(${this.#key.token.description ?? "anonymous"})`;
	}
}

const BACKGROUND_CONTEXT          = new EmptyContext("[Context BACKGROUND_CONTEXT]");
const TODO_CONTEXT          = new EmptyContext("[Context TODO_CONTEXT]");

function createContextKey   (description        )                {
	return Object.freeze({ token: Symbol(description) });
}

/** Derive a context containing one additional or replaced value. */
function withContextValue   (key               , value   , parent         )          {
	return new ContextValue(parent, key, value);
}

/**
 * Derive a context cancelled by either the parent signal or the supplied signal.
 * The parent context remains unchanged.
 */
function withAbortSignal(signal             , context         )          {
	const parentSignal = context.abortSignal;
	const combined = parentSignal === undefined ? signal : AbortSignal.any([parentSignal, signal]);
	return withContextValue(ABORT_SIGNAL_CONTEXT_KEY, combined, context);
}

/** Derive a context retaining all values except caller cancellation. Intended for mandatory cleanup only. */
function withoutAbortSignal(context         )          {
	return withContextValue(ABORT_SIGNAL_CONTEXT_KEY, undefined, context);
}

/** Derive an independently cancellable child context. */
function withCancel(context         )   
	                          
	                                            
  {
	const controller = new AbortController();
	return {
		context: withAbortSignal(controller.signal, context),
		cancel: (reason          ) => controller.abort(reason),
	};
}

/**
 * Observe a promise until it settles or the invocation is cancelled.
 * Cancellation rejects only this waiter; it does not cancel the underlying promise.
 */
function awaitWithContext   (promise            , context         )             {
	const signal = context.abortSignal;
	if (signal === undefined) return promise;
	if (signal.aborted) return Promise.reject(abortError(signal));
	return new Promise   ((resolve, reject) => {
		const onAbort = ()       => reject(abortError(signal));
		signal.addEventListener("abort", onAbort, { once: true });
		void promise.then(
			(value) => {
				signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
				resolve(value);
			},
			(error         ) => {
				signal.removeEventListener("abort", onAbort);
				reject(error);
			},
		);
	});
}

function abortError(signal             )        {
	const reason          = signal.reason;
	return reason instanceof Error ? reason : new DOMException("The operation was aborted", "AbortError");
}

// ══ state.ts ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
/**
 * Replicated state: an immutable JSON value with ordered, serialized change notifications. Either authoritative and
 * mutable (`replicatedState(initial)`) or a publication-only view of an external revision source
 * (`replicatedState(source)`).
 */



                                          
	                                    
	                          
 

                                     
	   
	                                                                                                            
	                                                                                                             
	   
	                              
	   
	                                                                                                          
	                                                                                                         
	                                                                                                         
	                                                                                                              
	   
	                                                                                                                  
	                                                                                                         
 

                                                                                      
	                  
	   
	                                                                                                              
	                                                                                                               
	   
	                                                                  
	   
	                                                                                
	                                                                   
	   
	                                          
 

/** One immutable authoritative revision committed after an attachment snapshot. */
                                                
	                                                                                               
	                        
	                                                         
	                  
	                                                                                                    
	                            
	                          
 

                                                     
	                                                                           
	                                                                  
	   
	                                                                                                 
	                                                                                                        
	                                                                                             
	   
	                                                                         
	                                                                               
	                
 

/**
 * An authoritative immutable revision source.
 *
 * `attach()` must synchronously and atomically capture one snapshot and register the returned attachment to buffer
 * every later committed frame. The snapshot must include every commit before that boundary; buffered frames must
 * include every commit after it, with no overlap or gap. Snapshot values, frame values, and operation batches are
 * immutable and remain valid after delivery. Chord only publishes these references; it never applies or re-diffs them.
 */
                                           
	                                             
 

                                               
	                                                                                                        
	                                          
 

/** A synchronously hydrated publication-only state backed by one source attachment. */
                                                                        
	                  
	                                                                                             
	                
 

/** The void signature also accepts synchronous callbacks that return an ignored value. */
                                                                                                

                         
	                  
	                          
	                                           
  

/** One public subscription, independent of producer and other subscriber progress. */
class StateSubscriber    {
	         #listener                  ;
	         #reportError                        ;
	         #pending                     = [];
	#running = false;
	#started = false;
	#closed = false;

	constructor(listener                  , reportError                        ) {
		this.#listener = listener;
		this.#reportError = reportError;
	}

	push(frame                  )       {
		if (this.#closed) return;
		if (this.#pending.length === 100) {
			// A cold replica can queue updates reentrantly before this subscriber's first hydration starts.
			const hydration = this.#started ? undefined : this.#pending[0];
			this.#pending.length = 0;
			if (hydration !== undefined) this.#pending.push(hydration);
		}
		this.#pending.push(frame);
	}

	drain()       {
		if (this.#running || this.#closed) return;
		this.#running = true;
		for (let frame = this.#pending.shift(); frame !== undefined; frame = this.#pending.shift()) {
			this.#started = true;
			try {
				const result          = this.#listener(frame.value, frame.context, frame.delivery);
				if (isPromiseLike(result)) {
					void Promise.resolve(result).then(
						() => this.#resume(),
						(error         ) => {
							this.#report(error);
							this.#resume();
						},
					);
					return;
				}
			} catch (error) {
				this.#report(error);
			}
		}
		this.#running = false;
	}

	clear()       {
		this.#pending.length = 0;
	}

	close()       {
		this.#closed = true;
		this.clear();
	}

	#resume()       {
		this.#running = false;
		this.drain();
	}

	#report(error         )       {
		try {
			this.#reportError(toError(error));
		} catch (reportError) {
			reportErrorAsync(toError(reportError));
		}
	}
}

                       
	                  
	                          
	                          
  

/** Maintains local publication order independently of how revisions are produced. */
class ReplicatedStatePublisher    {
	         #listeners = new Map                            ();
	         #reportError                        ;
	         #publications                   = [];
	#value   ;
	#sequence = 0;
	#delivering = false;

	constructor(initial   , reportError                         = reportErrorAsync) {
		this.#value = initial;
		this.#reportError = reportError;
	}

	get value()    {
		return this.#value;
	}

	snapshot()                                                   {
		return { value: this.#value, sequence: this.#sequence };
	}

	subscribe(listener                  )             {
		const { value, sequence } = this.snapshot();
		const subscriber = new StateSubscriber(listener, this.#reportError);
		this.#listeners.set(subscriber, sequence);
		// Hydration has no caller; it is delivered with the background context.
		subscriber.push({ value, context: BACKGROUND_CONTEXT, delivery: { kind: "hydrate", sequence } });
		subscriber.drain();
		return () => {
			subscriber.close();
			this.#listeners.delete(subscriber);
		};
	}

	/** Publish an already-prepared immutable revision. Subscriber failures are reported, never thrown. */
	publish(value   , context         )       {
		this.#value = value;
		this.#sequence += 1;
		this.#publications.push({ value, sequence: this.#sequence, context });
		if (this.#delivering) return;

		this.#delivering = true;
		try {
			for (
				let publication = this.#publications.shift();
				publication !== undefined;
				publication = this.#publications.shift()
			) {
				const delivery = { kind: "update", sequence: publication.sequence }         ;
				for (const [subscriber, hydratedSequence] of [...this.#listeners]) {
					if (publication.sequence <= hydratedSequence) continue;
					subscriber.push({ value: publication.value, context: publication.context, delivery });
					subscriber.drain();
				}
			}
		} finally {
			this.#delivering = false;
		}
	}
}

class MutableReplicatedStateImpl                                                        {
	         #tracker            ;
	         #publisher                             ;
	#changing = false;

	constructor(initial   ) {
		this.#tracker = track(initial);
		this.#publisher = new ReplicatedStatePublisher(this.#tracker.value);
	}

	get value()    {
		return this.#tracker.value;
	}

	change(context         , mutate                                                    )       {
		if (this.#changing) throw new Error("Replicated state cannot be changed reentrantly from a change callback");
		this.#changing = true;
		let prepared             ;
		try {
			const change = this.#tracker.beginChange();
			try {
				const outcome = (mutate                                           )(change.state);
				if (isPromiseLike(outcome)) {
					void Promise.resolve(outcome).catch(() => undefined);
					throw new TypeError("Replicated state change callbacks must be synchronous");
				}
				prepared = change.prepare();
			} catch (error) {
				change.abort();
				throw error;
			}
		} finally {
			this.#changing = false;
		}

		this.#tracker.adopt(prepared);
		if (prepared.ops.length > 0) this.#publisher.publish(prepared.value, context);
	}

	replace(context         , value   )       {
		if (this.#changing) throw new Error("Replicated state cannot be replaced from a change callback");
		const prepared = this.#tracker.prepareReplace(value);
		this.#tracker.adopt(prepared);
		if (prepared.ops.length > 0) this.#publisher.publish(prepared.value, context);
	}

	subscribe(listener                  )             {
		return this.#publisher.subscribe(listener);
	}
}

class AttachedReplicatedStateImpl                                          {
	         #publisher                             ;
	         #attachment                                    ;
	         #reportError                        ;
	#cursor        ;
	#disposed = false;

	constructor(attachment                                    , options                              ) {
		const { snapshot } = attachment;
		assertCursor(snapshot.cursor, "snapshot");
		this.#attachment = attachment;
		this.#cursor = snapshot.cursor;
		this.#reportError = options.onError ?? reportErrorAsync;
		this.#publisher = new ReplicatedStatePublisher(snapshot.value, (error) => this.#report(error));
	}

	get value()    {
		return this.#publisher.value;
	}

	subscribe(listener                  )             {
		return this.#publisher.subscribe(listener);
	}

	activate()       {
		this.#attachment.activate((frame) => this.#receive(frame));
	}

	dispose()       {
		if (this.#disposed) return;
		this.#disposed = true;
		this.#attachment.dispose();
	}

	#receive(frame                               )       {
		if (this.#disposed) return;
		try {
			assertCursor(frame.cursor, "frame");
			const expected = this.#cursor + 1;
			if (frame.cursor !== expected) {
				throw new Error(`Replicated state source cursor has a gap: expected ${expected}, received ${frame.cursor}`);
			}
			this.#cursor = frame.cursor;
			this.#publisher.publish(frame.value, frame.context);
		} catch (error) {
			this.#fail(toError(error));
		}
	}

	#fail(error       )       {
		if (this.#disposed) return;
		this.#disposed = true;
		try {
			this.#attachment.dispose();
		} catch (disposeError) {
			this.#report(new AggregateError([error, disposeError], "Replicated state source contract failed"));
			return;
		}
		this.#report(error);
	}

	#report(error         )       {
		try {
			this.#reportError(toError(error));
		} catch (reportError) {
			reportErrorAsync(toError(reportError));
		}
	}
}

/** Attach a publication-only replicated state to one authoritative immutable source stream. */
function attachReplicatedStateSource   (
	source                          ,
	options                               = {},
)                             {
	const attachment = source.attach();
	try {
		const state = new AttachedReplicatedStateImpl(attachment, options);
		state.activate();
		return state;
	} catch (error) {
		try {
			attachment.dispose();
		} catch (disposeError) {
			throw new AggregateError([error, disposeError], "Failed to attach replicated state source");
		}
		throw error;
	}
}

function assertCursor(cursor        , kind                      )       {
	if (!Number.isSafeInteger(cursor))
		throw new TypeError(`Replicated state source ${kind} cursor must be a safe integer`);
}

function isPromiseLike(value         )                                {
	return (
		((typeof value === "object" && value !== null) || typeof value === "function") &&
		typeof (value                      ).then === "function"
	);
}

function reportErrorAsync(error       )       {
	queueMicrotask(() => {
		throw error;
	});
}

function toError(error         )        {
	return error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error));
}

                                   
	                                 
	                                       
                              
/**
 * Create authoritative state by taking immutable ownership of an alias-free strict-JSON root.
 * The caller must not mutate `initial` after this call.
 */
                                                                                         
function replicatedState(
	initialOrSource                                         ,
	options                               ,
)                                                                    {
	if (isReplicatedStateSource(initialOrSource)) return attachReplicatedStateSource(initialOrSource, options);
	return new MutableReplicatedStateImpl(initialOrSource);
}

function isReplicatedStateSource(value        )                                          {
	return typeof (value                                 ).attach === "function";
}

// ══ websocket.ts ════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
/** A WebSocket server (e.g. `ws`'s `WebSocketServer`), structurally: Chord does not depend on `ws`. */
                                               
	                                                                                              
 

/** WebSocket peers are always accepted as clients: no roots, no epochs, no reload votes, not addressable. */
                                                                                                             
	                                                                                            
	                                                                                    
 

/** Accepts every connection of `server` on `host` as a client link stamped with the authenticated principal. */
function acceptWebSockets         (
	server                              ,
	host      ,
	options                                 ,
)       {
	server.on("connection", (socket, request) => {
		// Wrap at once so frames sent while authenticating are queued, not lost. Large payloads go out deflated; clients
		// may not send packed frames (a small message could inflate to anything).
		const link = webSocketLink(socket, { compress: true, acceptPacked: false });
		Promise.resolve(options.principal(request)).then(
			(principal) => {
				if (principal === undefined) link.close("unauthorized");
				else host.accept(link, { ...options, principal, kind: "client" });
			},
			() => link.close("authentication failed"),
		);
	});
}
